01. persistent-orbital-feedback-laboratory

打开实际网页 ↗

创建题 · worker timed_out · 历史运行 PASS · 质量分 86.2/100

验收项 7 · 场景 4 · 必做 4 · 复杂度候选分位 0.973

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Build a standalone Three.js browser app called “Orbital Feedback Laboratory.” Use only local starter dependencies and procedural geometry/materials; make no runtime network requests and load no models, textures, fonts, or shader files. Use Y-up world units and numeric tolerance 1e-5. Expose `window.orbitFeedback` with `scene`, `camera`, `renderer`, `controls`, `probes`, `getState()`, `step(count)`, `setPaused(value)`, `setSpeed(id,value)`, `setRadius(id,value)`, `selectAt(clientX,clientY)`, `save()`, `load()`, and `reset()`.

1. Render an 800×600 canvas with a PerspectiveCamera at `(0,8,12)`, looking at the origin, plus OrbitControls. Add a central cylinder of radius 0.6 and height 1 centered at the origin. Create three sphere probes with radius 0.22 and IDs `p0`, `p1`, `p2`. Their respective initial `(angle,radius,height,speed)` values are `(0,2,0.5,1)`, `(2π/3,2.8,1,-1.5)`, and `(4π/3,3.6,1.5,2)`. Angles are radians and speeds are radians per second. Give each probe a distinct solid color and a Line trail.

2. Maintain `time`, integer `frame`, and `paused`, initially `0`, `0`, and `false`. `step(count)` must synchronously advance exactly `count` fixed frames even while paused, using `dt=1/60`; reject non-integer or negative counts by returning `false` without mutation, otherwise return `true`. For each frame and probe, update `angle += speed*dt`, then set world position to `(radius*cos(angle),height,radius*sin(angle))`. Increment `time` by `dt` and `frame` by one. Append each resulting position to that probe’s trail, retaining at most 120 points including the current point. Continuous rendering may advance time only while unpaused and must use the same fixed-step rule.

3. `setPaused(value)` stores a strict boolean. `setSpeed` accepts finite values in `[-4,4]`; `setRadius` accepts finite values in `[1.5,4]`. Both return `true` on success. Unknown IDs, non-finite values, or out-of-range values return `false` and leave all state unchanged. Radius changes affect the next computed position; they do not rewrite existing trail points.

4. Implement `selectAt` with a Raycaster and canvas-relative pointer coordinates derived from the canvas bounding rectangle. A hit on a probe sets `selectedId` to its ID and gives only that probe emissive intensity 0.8; a miss sets `selectedId=null` and all emissive intensities to 0. Return the selected ID or `null`.

5. `getState()` returns a deep-copy JSON-safe snapshot containing `version:1`, time, frame, paused, selectedId, every probe’s angle/radius/height/speed/current position, and all retained trail points. `save()` writes that snapshot to localStorage key `orbitFeedback.v1` and returns the snapshot. `load()` restores a valid saved snapshot, including trails, pause, and selection, returning `true`; malformed JSON, wrong version, missing fields, or invalid numeric values return `false` without changing live state. Automatically attempt `load()` at startup.

6. Provide Pause, Save, Load, and Reset buttons and a text readout of frame, time, paused, and selectedId. `reset()` removes the storage key and restores every initial value, including empty selection, cleared trails, default camera and controls, and unpaused state; it must not merely reload the page.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 86.2/100

Worker 超时、网页运行状态和质量分是独立记录。此处保留原始 VLM 评分；预览部署不改变评分。

维度分数理由
instruction3/4All core scene, stepped simulation, selection, persistence, and UI flows are present. Reset's running-state trail behavior keeps it from fully satisfying the requirement that trails be cleared after reset.
spatial4/4Geometry, probe scales and heights, shadows, central occlusion, orbit parallax, and distinct materials consistently convey the orbital laboratory over multiple tested views.
interaction3/4Pause, selection, save/load, reset, and keyboard buttons respond, and invalid operations recover. Reset recovery is undermined by immediate automatic trail accumulation after reporting cleared initial conditions.
visual4/4The polished scientific-instrument presentation, clear telemetry hierarchy, coherent restrained palette, and legible responsive layout support the task throughout tested states.
stability3/4Reload, repeated persistence, responsive layouts, and all local assets worked; only a harmless favicon 404 appeared. The reset/running trail inconsistency is a local state issue rather than a crash or load failure.

02. deterministic-keyframe-clip-foundry

打开实际网页 ↗

创建题 · worker completed · 历史运行 PASS · 质量分 63.8/100

验收项 6 · 场景 4 · 必做 4 · 复杂度候选分位 0.862

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Build a standalone Three.js browser page called “Keyframe Clip Foundry.” Use only local starter Three.js code, procedural geometry, solid-color materials, inline JavaScript, and ordinary DOM. Load no models, textures, fonts, animation libraries, or remote data, and make no runtime network requests. Render continuously, but advance animation only through the API below.

1. Use meters, Y-up coordinates, and seconds. Create a rig named `rig` at world origin with a torso mesh named `torso` using BoxGeometry(1, 1.4, 0.6) at local (0, 1.4, 0). Add a Group named `shoulder` at local (0.75, 1.9, 0), containing an `upperArm` BoxGeometry(0.3, 1, 0.3) mesh at local (0, -0.5, 0). Add a ground grid and a perspective camera at (4, 3, 6) looking at (0, 1.2, 0). Provide DOM readouts for clip, time, playing state, fade progress, and event count.

2. Store these inline clips as plain JavaScript data. `idle`: duration 2, loop true, root Y track times [0,1,2] values [0,0.1,0], arm Z track values [0,0,0]. `walk`: duration 1, loop true, root Y times [0,0.5,1] values [0,0.08,0], arm Z values [0,0.6,0]. `wave`: duration 1.5, loop false, root Y times [0,0.75,1.5] values [0,0,0], arm Z values [0,2.4,2.4]. Root Y means `rig.position.y`; arm Z means `shoulder.rotation.z` in radians. Sample each track with linear interpolation between surrounding keys and endpoint clamping. Looping clips use time modulo duration, so sampling exactly at duration gives the first key; non-looping clips clamp at their final key.

3. Expose `window.clipLab` with `seek(t)`, `step(frames)`, `play()`, `pause()`, `setClip(name, fadeSeconds)`, `reset()`, and `getState()`. Start paused on `idle` at time 0. `seek(t)` sets the current clip time and samples immediately. `step(frames)` advances exactly `frames/60` seconds even while paused; `play()` enables automatic fixed 1/60-second advancement per rendered frame and `pause()` disables it. A finished non-looping clip automatically pauses at its duration. Reject non-finite or negative times, frame counts, and fade durations with `RangeError` without changing state.

4. `setClip(name, fadeSeconds)` switches to the named clip at target time 0. With fade 0, apply the target immediately. Otherwise freeze the complete outgoing sampled pose at transition start and blend it to the advancing target pose using `u=clamp(fadeElapsed/fadeSeconds,0,1)` and weight `s=u*u*(3-2*u)`; interpolate root Y and arm Z as `outgoing*(1-s)+target*s`. The target clock and fade clock both advance only through normal time advancement. An unknown clip name throws `RangeError` without changing state.

5. Emit `wave-start` when `wave` is entered and `wave-peak` when its local time first reaches or passes 0.75. Keep an ordered event log and expose it in `getState()`, along with clip, time, playing, finished, fadeElapsed, fadeSeconds, rootY, and armZ. `reset()` restores the initial paused idle pose, clears the log, and cancels any fade. Invalid input must not append events.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 63.8/100

Worker 超时、网页运行状态和质量分是独立记录。此处保留原始 VLM 评分；预览部署不改变评分。

维度分数理由
instruction2/4The main clip lab flows work, but substantial specified contracts fail: idle interpolation magnitudes, walk looping-at-duration semantics, fade interpolation, and seek during fade. The required API, readouts, events, and reset are otherwise present in one coherent page.
interaction2/4Single actions, playback, stepping, and reset work, but the required sequence and fade-boundary contracts fail, including non-advancing fade state after seek. Some failures are only disclosed by numeric state probes, although the UI presents clear feedback for the flows it does implement.
spatial3/4The rig reads clearly in 3D with plausible scale and joints, and rotation/pose changes are visible with coherent lighting and shadows. Exact geometry and hierarchy could not be probed; the large mobile readout also obscures the upper rig.
stability3/4Loading and reload are clean with no console errors and only local successful requests; repeated playback, reset, and viewport changes did not crash. The broken fade update under seek is a local nonblocking contract issue, and mobile presentation is obstructed.
visual3/4Desktop composition, typography, color, and 3D form are coherent and legible. State changes improve understanding, but mobile overlays the rig and some secondary UI text is faint.
Common rubric：本批通用检查与评分锚点

03. deterministic-exclusion-shard-field-lab

打开实际网页 ↗

创建题 · worker completed · 历史运行 PASS · 质量分 72.5/100

验收项 6 · 场景 4 · 必做 4 · 复杂度候选分位 0.914

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Build a standalone Three.js browser page called “Exclusion Shard Field Lab.” Use only the clean starter’s local Three.js and OrbitControls, procedural geometry, solid-color materials, inline CSS/JavaScript, and ordinary DOM. Load no models, textures, fonts, animation libraries, or remote assets and make no runtime network requests. Use Y-up world units, XYZ Euler order, radians, and numeric tolerance 1e-5.

1. Create a full-window scene with PerspectiveCamera(60, aspect, 0.1, 100) at (0,0,8), looking at the origin, and non-auto-rotating OrbitControls. Add ambient and directional lighting. Create `core`, an IcosahedronGeometry(1,1) mesh at the origin, and `group`, containing exactly 125 BoxGeometry(0.1,0.1,0.1) meshes. Iterate x, then y, then z, each from -2 through 2; name each cube `cube_x_y_z`, position it at (x,y,z), and set its rotation to (0.1*x,0.1*y,0.1*z).

2. Expose `window.shardFieldLab` with `scene`, `camera`, `renderer`, `controls`, `core`, `group`, `scatter(seed)`, `step(dt)`, `reset()`, and `getState()`. `getState()` returns `{seed, elapsed, scattering}` plus arrays of each cube’s current position, start position, and target position in group-child order. Also store the current transition’s start and target as `cube.userData.scatterStart` and `cube.userData.scatterTarget`.

3. `scatter(seed)` must generate a complete replacement layout without using Math.random. Set the PRNG state to `seed >>> 0`; for every draw update it with `state = (1664525 * state + 1013904223) >>> 0` and return `state / 4294967296`. Process cubes in group-child order. Each attempt consumes three draws and proposes `(5*(u1-0.5), 5*(u2-0.5), 5*(u3-0.5))`. Accept only if its distance from the origin is at least 2 and its distance from every already accepted target is at least 0.35. Allow 4000 attempts per cube; on exhaustion, throw an Error and leave the entire existing layout and transition state unchanged.

4. A successful scatter records all starts and targets before moving anything, sets `elapsed=0` and `scattering=true`, and animates for one second per cube. Cube i has delay `0.05*(i%5)`. During `step(dt)`, local time is `clamp(elapsed-delay,0,1)`, interpolation weight is `q=t*t*(3-2*t)`, and position is `start+(target-start)*q`. At local time 1, assign the target exactly. Set `scattering=false` only when elapsed is at least 1.2. Ignore negative, NaN, or infinite dt; otherwise add dt to elapsed. Also set core rotation to `(0.2*elapsed,0.3*elapsed,0)` on every accepted step. Do not advance simulation from wall-clock time.

5. `reset()` restores the exact initial lattice positions and rotations, core rotation (0,0,0), seed 0, elapsed 0, and scattering false; clear each cube’s scatter start and target to null. It must not recreate geometry, reset the camera, or alter controls. Provide Scatter and Reset buttons; Scatter reads an integer seed from a numeric input whose default is 12345.

6. Render continuously and handle resize by updating camera aspect, projection matrix, and renderer size. All motion and layout changes must come only from the specified API or buttons, so repeated seeds and step sequences produce identical states.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 72.5/100

Worker 超时、网页运行状态和质量分是独立记录。此处保留原始 VLM 评分；预览部署不改变评分。

维度分数理由
instruction2/4Most scene and API requirements are delivered, but the requested UI-driven one-second scatter animation is absent because elapsed never advances from wall-clock time. The required exhaustion error/recovery is also incorrect for seed 0. These are substantial requested-flow defects rather than minor omissions.
interaction2/4Scatter and Reset produce scene/state changes, and API-controlled interpolation and invalid dt handling are correct. But the central scatter interaction does not animate or visibly progress over time, and the seed-0 exhaustion recovery path is missing.
spatial4/4The faceted core, 125 small rotated boxes, ambient/directional lighting, depth, and orbit viewpoints are consistently coherent. Deterministic targets satisfy both exclusion constraints, and reset returns the exact lattice without displacing the camera or recreating geometry.
stability3/4Repeated operations, resize, orbit, reset, and reload remained stable with no post-reload console errors or remote requests. The layout works at both tested viewport sizes. The nonblocking favicon 404 and the unhandled seed-0 sampling failure are local issues; the UI-animation time failure has higher-level functional impact.
visual3/4The composition, typography, dark palette, blue core, and gray shards form a coherent field-lab presentation. The status panel is legible on desktop and mobile, though the static scatter makes the status presentation less useful during interaction.

04. persistent-scroll-chair-configurator

打开实际网页 ↗

创建题 · worker completed · 历史运行 PASS · 质量分 86.2/100

验收项 6 · 场景 4 · 必做 4 · 复杂度候选分位 0.908

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Build a standalone Three.js browser app called “Scroll Chair Studio.” Use only local starter dependencies and procedural geometry; make no runtime network requests. Use meters, Y-up coordinates, degrees for rotation inputs, and numeric tolerance 1e-5. Expose `window.chairStudio` with `scene`, `camera`, `renderer`, `controls`, `chairRoot`, `upholstery`, `getState()`, `setScroll(p)`, `setColor(id)`, `setRotation(degrees)`, `enterCustomize()`, `exitCustomize()`, `save()`, `load()`, `importState(text)`, and `reset()`.

1. Create a responsive canvas, ambient and directional lighting, a 12×12 ground plane at y=0, and a PerspectiveCamera(45, aspect, 0.1, 100). Build `chairRoot` with a seat BoxGeometry(2,0.3,1.8) named `seat` at (0,1,0), a back BoxGeometry(2,1.8,0.25) named `back` at (0,1.9,-0.775), and four BoxGeometry(0.18,1,0.18) legs at x=±0.8, z=±0.65, y=0.5. Seat and back must share one MeshStandardMaterial named `upholstery`; legs use a separate dark material. Initial color is green #96b2a1, rotation is 0, and mode is browse.

2. In browse mode, controls are disabled and `setScroll(p)` clamps finite p to [0,1], then positions the camera by piecewise linear interpolation while targeting (0,1,0). For 0≤p≤0.5, interpolate from (0,2,6) to (0,1.5,3.25) using u=2p. For 0.5<p≤1, interpolate from (0,1.5,3.25) to (-3,1.5,0) using u=2p-1. Non-finite p returns false without mutation; valid calls return true. Provide a range input min=0, max=1, step=0.001 wired to this method.

3. `enterCustomize()` sets mode to customize, enables OrbitControls targeting (0,1,0), and places the camera at (-1.95,2.54,2.29). `exitCustomize()` returns to browse, disables controls, and recomputes the camera from the retained scroll value. `setRotation(degrees)` clamps finite input to [-45,45] and sets `chairRoot.rotation.y` to that angle in radians; non-finite input returns false without mutation. `setColor(id)` accepts only green #96b2a1, red #a57d7d, or violet #aa83aa, updates the shared upholstery material, and returns true; unknown IDs return false without mutation. Add Customize, Exit, and three labeled swatch buttons.

4. `getState()` returns `{version:1,mode,scroll,colorId,rotation,cameraPosition,target,controlsEnabled}`, with cameraPosition and target as three-number arrays and rotation in degrees. `save()` writes that state, excluding derived camera fields and controlsEnabled, to localStorage key `scroll-chair-studio-v1` as JSON with exactly version, mode, scroll, colorId, and rotation, and returns the JSON string. `load()` returns false without mutation if no save exists; otherwise it applies a valid save, derives the appropriate camera and controls state, and returns true.

5. `importState(text)` accepts the same five-field JSON schema. Reject malformed JSON, wrong version, non-finite or out-of-range scroll/rotation, unknown mode/colorId, or non-number numeric fields by returning false with no state or scene mutation. Valid imports return true and update the scene. `reset()` removes the saved key and restores browse mode, scroll 0, green, rotation 0, and the initial camera; it does not reload the page.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 86.2/100

05. deterministic-survey-rig-and-center-ray-lab

创建题 · worker completed · 历史运行 PASS · 质量分 83.8/100

验收项 6 · 场景 4 · 必做 4 · 复杂度候选分位 0.868

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Build a standalone Three.js browser app called “Survey Rig Lab.” Use only local starter dependencies, procedural geometry, solid-color materials, and ordinary DOM/JavaScript. Make no runtime network requests and load no models, textures, fonts, or controls libraries. Use meters, Y-up coordinates, radians internally, and numeric tolerance 1e-5. Expose `window.surveyRig` with `scene`, `camera`, `renderer`, `yaw`, `pitch`, `targets`, `setInput(state)`, `step(dt)`, `look(dx,dy,shiftHeld)`, `pickCenter()`, `reset()`, and `getState()`.

1. Create an 800×600 canvas and a `PerspectiveCamera(60, 800/600, 0.1, 200)` whose default forward direction is local -Z. Add `yaw` to the scene at (0,0,0), add `pitch` as its child, and add the camera as pitch's child at local (0,1.6,0). Add a 40×40 ground plane at y=0 and a grid. Create three 1×1×1 box targets with IDs `A`, `B`, and `C`, centered respectively at (0,1.6,-6), (4,1.6,-6), and (-4,1.6,-8). Store them in `targets` in that order.

2. `setInput(state)` replaces the six Boolean flags `forward`, `backward`, `left`, `right`, `up`, and `down`; omitted flags become false. `step(dt)` accepts only finite dt in [0,1], otherwise throws `RangeError` without changing state. For horizontal input, x=right-left and z=backward-forward; normalize (x,z) if its length exceeds 1. Translate yaw locally by `yaw.translateX(4*x*dt)` and `yaw.translateZ(4*z*dt)`. Change camera local y by `2*(up-down)*dt`, clamped to [0.5,4]. Use no inertia, damping, gravity, or automatic simulation advancement.

3. `look(dx,dy,shiftHeld)` does nothing when `shiftHeld` is false. Otherwise apply `yaw.rotation.y -= dx*0.002` and `pitch.rotation.x -= dy*0.002`, then clamp pitch to [-Math.PI/2,Math.PI/2]. Nonfinite dx or dy throws `TypeError` without changing either rotation. Bind WASD to horizontal flags, E/Q to up/down, and Shift+mousemove to the same look formula; keyboard flags change only on key events, and movement advances only through `step`.

4. `pickCenter()` raycasts from the camera through canvas NDC (0,0), intersects only the three target meshes, and returns null for no hit. Otherwise return `{id, point:[x,y,z], distance}` for the nearest hit and set that target's material color to 0xffcc00; restore every other target to 0x3388ff. Ground and grid must never be pickable.

5. `getState()` returns fresh data containing `yawPosition` as an array, `yawAngle`, `pitchAngle`, `eyeHeight`, all six input flags, and `selectedId`. `reset()` restores the exact initial transforms and target colors, clears all flags and selection, and leaves the renderer and scene objects in place. Provide Reset and Step buttons; Step calls `step(1/60)`. Show text readouts for yaw, pitch, eye height, and selected ID.

6. A render loop may render continuously but must not call `step` or otherwise advance movement. Resize handling must update camera aspect and renderer size without altering rig transforms. All API results must be deterministic and independent of frame rate.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 83.8/100

Worker 超时、网页运行状态和质量分是独立记录。此处保留原始 VLM 评分；预览部署不改变评分。

维度分数理由
instruction3/4Most specified flows and API contracts are delivered coherently, including picking, reset and UI Step. Minor-but-explicit contract omissions remain: invalid dt exception type for nonfinite values and the required resize camera-aspect/renderer-size updates.
interaction3/4Pointer picking, keyboard-to-step sequence, repeated Step, vertical boundary traversal, selection changes and Reset recovery work naturally with clear visual/readout feedback. The invalid-step exception-type defect is a local contract flaw rather than a blocked primary interaction.
spatial4/4Required hierarchy, units, dimensions, positions and camera perspective are precise. Multiple changed views show coherent depth, occlusion and parallax, with clear solid-color targets and a grounded survey grid.
stability3/4All standard tested interactions and reload were reliable, with only favicon 404 diagnostics. Mobile controls remain reachable. The required resize behavior is a tested local sizing flaw and invalid nonfinite dt uses the wrong exception type; neither prevents normal use.
visual3/4The composition, monospaced typography, restrained palette and instrument-style panels form a coherent expression. Yellow selection and green input feedback aid state recognition. The unchanged renderer aspect causes scale/fit inconsistencies across viewport sizes.

06. deterministic-lightning-fracture-laboratory

打开实际网页 ↗

创建题 · worker timed_out · 历史运行 PASS · 质量分 57.5/100

验收项 6 · 场景 4 · 必做 4 · 复杂度候选分位 0.935

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Build a standalone Three.js browser page called “Lightning Fracture Lab.” Use only local Three.js/OrbitControls code, procedural BufferGeometry, solid-color materials, and ordinary DOM. Make no runtime network requests and load no models, textures, fonts, audio, shader files, or remote data. Use Y-up world units and expose `window.stormLab` with `generate(seed)`, `strike(seed)`, `step(milliseconds)`, `reset()`, and `getState()`.

1. Render an 800×600 canvas with a perspective camera at `(0,18,55)`, field of view 50 degrees, looking at `(0,12,0)`. Add a 40×40 grid on the XZ plane at `y=0`. The bolt starts at `(0,30,0)` and ends at `(0,0,0)`. Use a white main bolt, cyan branches and crackles, and a white impact sphere. OrbitControls may inspect the scene, but simulation time must advance only through `step`.

2. `generate(seed)` must replace existing effects and construct one deterministic bolt. Use a 32-bit unsigned LCG: `state=(1664525*state+1013904223)>>>0`, beginning with `state=seed>>>0`; each random value is `state/4294967296`. Build the main path by recursive midpoint displacement to depth 4, processing the first half before the second half. At each internal node, consume one random value, set `m=(a+b)/2`, `d=b-a`, `p=normalize((-d.y,d.x,0))`, and replace `m` by `m+p*((random-0.5)*D)`. Initial `D=6`; halve `D` for each child recursion. Keep every main-path z coordinate zero. This yields 17 ordered points and 16 main segments.

3. Add two branches, each a three-point polyline. Branch A runs from main point 4 to that point plus `(-6,-8,2)`, with its midpoint offset by `(0,0,1)`. Branch B runs from main point 8 to that point plus `(5,-6,-2)`, with its midpoint offset by `(0,0,-1)`. Flatten the main path followed by branches A and B into one `THREE.LineSegments` geometry: 20 segments and 40 position vertices. `getState()` must return the seed, main points, segment count, draw-range count, opacity, simulation time, phase, impact scale, and crackle count.

4. Create six ground crackles at `y=0.02`. For `i=0…5`, let `a=i*Math.PI/3`; each has inner point `(2cos(a),0.02,2sin(a))`, outer point `(6cos(a),0.02,6sin(a))`, and midpoint at radius 4 and angle `a+(i%2===0?0.12:-0.12)`. Each crackle contributes two line segments, for 12 total. Add an impact sphere centered at the origin with base radius 1.

5. `strike(seed)` performs `generate(seed)`, hides crackles and sphere, sets time 0 and phase `drawing`, and sets bolt opacity 1. During `0≤t≤400`, reveal `floor(20*clamp(t/400,0,1))` segments and set draw-range count to twice that number. At `t=400`, show all bolt segments, crackles, and sphere. For `400<t<800`, set sphere scale uniformly to `8*sin(Math.PI*(t-400)/400)`. Keep opacity 1 through `t=600`; for `600<t<800`, use `1-(t-600)/200`. At `t≥800`, remove all effects, set phase `idle`, draw-range count and impact scale to 0, and crackle count to 0. Clamp large `step` inputs at the final state.

6. Provide a seed text input plus Generate, Strike, Step 200 ms, and Reset buttons. A valid seed is an integer from 0 through 4294967295. Invalid input must leave the current scene unchanged and display `Invalid seed`; valid actions clear that message. `reset()` removes every effect, restores time 0, phase `idle`, opacity 1, and seed 0, clears the message, and leaves camera and grid unchanged.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 57.5/100

Worker 超时、网页运行状态和质量分是独立记录。此处保留原始 VLM 评分；预览部署不改变评分。

维度分数理由
instruction3/4Nearly all requested state and control flows are delivered coherently, including deterministic generation, the stepped lifecycle, invalid-input recovery, reset, local assets, and public stormLab methods. The key omission is rendered delivery of the white main lightning bolt.
interaction3/4The advertised buttons, keyboard invalid-input flow, repeated stepping, boundary completion, generation, and reset all function. Feedback is slightly incomplete because the readouts advance the bolt draw range without showing the corresponding bolt in the scene.
spatial1/4The grid, radial crackles, and impact sphere demonstrate real perspective 3D with three coherent orbit views. Nevertheless, the principal scene subject, the main lightning path from y=30 to ground, is absent from every render, leaving a severe spatial/content error.
stability4/4Desktop and mobile layouts loaded reliably, all controls remained reachable, repeated actions reached the idle boundary, reset recovered the initial state, and reload was clean. The only diagnostic issue was a nonblocking local favicon 404.
visual2/4Typography, palette, readouts, and responsive controls form a readable technical-lab style, while the oversized empty center and invisible lightning leave the primary visual hierarchy and intended dramatic composition unfulfilled.
Common rubric：本批通用检查与评分锚点

07. deterministic-parametric-flipbook-laboratory

打开实际网页 ↗

创建题 · worker completed · 历史运行 PASS · 质量分 100.0/100

验收项 6 · 场景 4 · 必做 4 · 复杂度候选分位 0.930

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Build a standalone Three.js browser app called “Flipbook Lab.” Use only local Three.js and procedural geometry/materials; make no runtime network requests and load no textures, models, fonts, or animation libraries. Use Y-up world units, radians, and numeric tolerance 1e-5. Expose window.flipbookLab with scene, camera, renderer, book, sheets, setProgress(p), getProgress(), getGoal(), flipTo(target), nextSheet(), previousSheet(), advance(dt), reset(), flipSheet(i), and pick(ndcX,ndcY).

1. Create a full-window scene with resize handling, a perspective camera (FOV 45, near 0.1, far 100) at (0,2.6,2.8) looking at the origin, ambient light, and directional light. Set book = new THREE.Group() and add four sheet groups named sheet-0 through sheet-3 in index order. Each sheet must contain a DoubleSide mesh made from PlaneGeometry(1.2,0.8,24,16), rotated X by -PI/2 and then translated X by 0.6, so its undeformed bounds are x=[0,1.2], y=0, z=[-0.4,0.4]. Give sheets distinct solid colors and store each zero-based index in sheet.userData.sheetIndex.

2. Define N=4 and spacing=0.01. setProgress(p) must immediately clamp finite p to [0,4], cancel any goal, and rebuild all sheet states. For sheet i, its flip q is 0 when i>floor(progress), 1 when i<floor(progress), and the fractional part f when i=floor(progress); at integer progress 4 every q=1. Set sheet.rotation.z=PI*q. Let rightY=-spacing*i, leftY=-spacing*(N-i), and yBlend=max(0,2*q-1); set sheet.position.y=rightY+yBlend*(leftY-rightY). Set book.position.x to -0.3+0.3*f when floor(progress)=0, 0.3*f when floor(progress)=N-1, 0.3 when progress=N, and 0 otherwise.

3. Deform every sheet mesh from its original undeformed coordinates on each rebuild: for original local x in [0,1.2], set local position.y=0.08*sin(PI*q)*sin(PI*x/1.2), retain x and z, and recompute vertex normals. Thus at progress 1.5 the center vertex of sheet-1 has local y=0.08, while completed and untouched sheets are flat. Do not accumulate deformation between calls.

4. flipTo(target) clamps a finite target to [0,4], stores it as the goal, and leaves progress unchanged until advance(dt) is called. advance(dt) ignores negative or non-finite dt and otherwise moves progress toward the goal at 2 sheet units per second without overshoot, rebuilding geometry and transforms after every movement. nextSheet() targets min(4,floor(progress+1e-9)+1); previousSheet() targets max(0,ceil(progress-1e-9)-1). reset() sets progress and goal to 0 and rebuilds immediately.

5. flipSheet(i) must do nothing for an invalid index; otherwise call flipTo(i) when progress>i, or flipTo(i+1) otherwise. pick(ndcX,ndcY) raycasts recursively against book using the supplied normalized device coordinates, identifies the first hit’s sheet ancestor, calls flipSheet for it, and returns its index; return null for no hit. Canvas pointer clicks must convert coordinates to NDC and use this same picking path.

6. Provide a progress slider (min 0, max 4, step 0.001), Next, Previous, and Reset buttons, and readouts for progress and goal. Slider input calls setProgress; buttons call the corresponding API. Keep controls synchronized after every API operation. Non-finite setProgress or flipTo inputs must be ignored without changing progress, goal, geometry, or transforms.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 100.0/100

08. deterministic-panorama-dwell-relay-lab

打开实际网页 ↗

创建题 · worker timed_out · 历史运行 PASS · 质量分 55.0/100

验收项 6 · 场景 4 · 必做 4 · 复杂度候选分位 0.911

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Build a standalone Three.js browser page called “Panorama Dwell Relay Lab.” Use only locally available Three.js code and procedural geometry/materials; load no images, models, fonts, controls libraries, or remote assets and make no runtime network requests. Use Y-up world units and expose `window.relayLab` with `scene`, `camera`, `renderer`, `rooms`, `markers`, `pick`, `setView`, `setDwell`, `step`, `activate`, `reset`, and `getState`.

1. Create a full-window scene and `PerspectiveCamera(60, aspect, 0.1, 1000)` at `(0,0,0)`. Add a radius-100 sphere with `BackSide` material as a non-pickable enclosure. Create room groups `A` and `B`, with only the current room visible. Each marker is a radius-2 `SphereGeometry(2,16,12)` mesh at distance 40 from the origin. Room A contains `alpha:(40,0,0)`, `beta:(0,20,-34.64101615137755)`, and `exit:(0,0,-40)`. Room B contains `return:(0,0,40)` and `target:(-40,0,0)`. Give markers distinct solid colors and set each mesh's `name` and `userData.id` to its ID.

2. Define view angles in degrees by `forward=(sin(yaw)*cos(pitch), sin(pitch), -cos(yaw)*cos(pitch))`, with the camera looking from the origin along that vector. Initial and reset view is yaw 0, pitch 0. `setView(yaw,pitch)` normalizes yaw to `[-180,180)`, clamps pitch to `[-80,80]`, updates the camera, and returns true. Non-finite inputs return false without changing state.

3. `pick(ndcX,ndcY)` raycasts only visible marker meshes, ignoring the enclosure and hidden room, and returns the nearest marker ID or null. Non-finite coordinates or coordinates outside `[-1,1]` return null. Picking alone must not activate anything. `activate(id)` returns false for an unknown or non-current-room ID; otherwise it records the ID in `activations`. For `alpha`, `beta`, and `target`, immediately aim at the marker using `yaw=atan2(x,-z)` and `pitch=asin(y/40)`, both converted to degrees. Activating `exit` selects room B and resets yaw/pitch to 0; activating `return` selects room A and resets yaw/pitch to 0.

4. Implement center-screen dwell selection without wall-clock timers. `setDwell(boolean)` enables or disables dwell and clears `dwellId` and `dwellMs`. While enabled, `step(ms)` picks at NDC `(0,0)`, accumulates milliseconds only while the same marker remains centered, and resets accumulation to zero whenever the centered ID changes or is null. At `dwellMs >= 1000`, activate that marker once, then disable dwell and clear dwell progress. Ignore negative or non-finite step durations.

5. `getState()` returns `{room,yaw,pitch,dwellEnabled,dwellId,dwellMs,activations}` with activation IDs in chronological order. `reset()` restores room A, yaw/pitch 0, disabled and cleared dwell, and an empty activation history; it must not recreate geometry or reset canvas size. Render continuously and resize the camera and renderer with the window.

6. Provide small text readouts for room, yaw, pitch, dwell ID/progress, and activation history, plus Reset and Dwell buttons. Pointer clicks on the canvas may call `pick` and `activate`, but all behavior must also be deterministic through the exposed API. Numeric comparisons use tolerance `1e-4`.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 55.0/100

Worker 超时、网页运行状态和质量分是独立记录。此处保留原始 VLM 评分；预览部署不改变评分。

09. deterministic-coin-tunnel-picking-lab

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Build a standalone Three.js browser page called “Coin Tunnel Lab.” Use only local starter dependencies and procedural geometry; make no runtime network requests and load no models, textures, fonts, or animation libraries. Use Y-up world units and numeric tolerance 1e-5.

1. Create a full-window scene with background 0x08080c, a PerspectiveCamera (fov 35, near 0.1, far 2000), and two white DirectionalLights of intensity 1.5 at (-1,0,0) and (1,0,0). Add a Group named `tunnel` containing eight coin Groups named `coin-0` through `coin-7`. Each coin must contain a gold MeshStandardMaterial CylinderGeometry(3,3,0.54,16), rotated X by PI/2, and a TorusGeometry(3,0.3,8,64) rim; both face along Z. Add a wireframe, open-ended CylinderGeometry(60,60,400,32,1,true) named `tunnel-shell`, but do not make it pickable.

2. Generate coin paths from this inline `[angle,q,initialProgress]` fixture: `[[0,0,0.10],[Math.PI/4,0.25,0.25],[Math.PI/2,0.5,0.40],[3*Math.PI/4,0.75,0.55],[Math.PI,1,0.70],[5*Math.PI/4,0.125,0.85],[3*Math.PI/2,0.375,0],[7*Math.PI/4,0.625,0.95]]`. For each coin, radius `r=12+48*sqrt(q)`, local position is `(r*cos(angle),200-400*p,r*sin(angle))`, and uniform scale is `min(1,p/0.05)`. Initial coin rotations and tunnel rotation are zero.

3. Expose `window.coinLab` with `scene`, `camera`, `renderer`, `tunnel`, `coins`, `reset()`, `step(n)`, `setProgress(i,p)`, `setView(name)`, `projectCoin(i)`, `pick(ndcX,ndcY)`, and `state()`. `step(n)` advances exactly n fixed steps: every coin gets `p+=0.0015`, rotation X `+=0.06`, and rotation Z `+=0.03`; if `p>1`, subtract 1 and set that coin’s X/Z rotations to zero. Then add 0.00025 to `tunnel.rotation.y` and update positions/scales. Reject non-integer n or n outside 0–10000 with RangeError and no mutation. `setProgress` rejects invalid indices or non-finite p outside [0,1] similarly.

4. `setView("inside")` sets camera up `(0,0,-1)`, position `(0,-200,0)`, and lookAt origin; `setView("overview")` sets up `(0,1,0)`, position `(0,0,650)`, and lookAt origin. Reject other names with RangeError. `projectCoin(i)` returns the coin center’s world-space projected `{x,y,z}` in NDC. `pick` raycasts only coin meshes, selects the nearest hit’s coin, returns its name, and on a miss returns null, sets selected to null, and resets every coin material’s emissive to 0x000000. Selection sets that coin’s materials’ emissive to 0x552200 and all others to 0x000000. Canvas pointer clicks must perform the same pick using canvas-bounds NDC.

5. Render continuously but never advance simulation automatically. Provide Step and Reset buttons plus text readouts for total steps, tunnel rotation, and selected coin. Step calls `step(1)`. `state()` returns `{steps,tunnelRotation,selected,coins:[{name,p,position,scale,rotation}]}` using current local transforms.

6. Reset restores the fixture progress values, zero coin/tunnel rotations, derived positions/scales, zero steps, null selection/emissives, and the inside camera view. It must not recreate geometry or add duplicate objects, lights, listeners, or animation loops.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 68.8/100

After stepping, groups tumble while rims remain radial, and overview coins become tiny and partially cropped.

visual3/4Typography, dark-gold color, telemetry, and tunnel treatment are coherent and readable at both widths. Weak coin silhouettes, tiny overview coins, sparse inside composition, and subtle selection emphasis are local visual defects that limit spatial legibility.

10. deterministic-bim-origin-and-picking-inspector

打开实际网页 ↗

创建题 · worker completed · 历史运行 PASS · 质量分 91.2/100

验收项 6 · 场景 4 · 必做 4 · 复杂度候选分位 0.919

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Build a standalone Three.js browser app called “BIM Origin Inspector.” Use only local Three.js code, procedural BoxGeometry, and solid-color materials; make no runtime network requests and load no models, textures, fonts, controls libraries, or remote data. Use meters, Y-up coordinates, an 800×600 canvas, and numeric tolerance 0.0001. Expose `window.bimOriginLab` with `scene`, `camera`, `renderer`, `modelRoot`, `objects`, `bounds`, `origin`, `selectedId`, `setTypeVisible(type, visible)`, `pick(ndcX, ndcY)`, `focusSelected()`, `reset()`, and `getState()`.

1. Create exactly four meshes from this inline fixture, storing each mesh in `objects` and setting `mesh.name` and `mesh.userData.id` to its ID and `mesh.userData.type` to its type: `wall-A`, IfcWall, center `[-4,1,0]`, size `[2,2,4]`; `slab-A`, IfcSlab, center `[0,0.5,0]`, size `[6,1,6]`; `window-A`, IfcWindow, center `[3,2,-1]`, size `[1,4,2]`; `beam-A`, IfcBeam, center `[0,4,3]`, size `[8,2,2]`. Sizes are full width, height, and depth. Put every mesh directly under `modelRoot`, using the listed centers as local positions.

2. Set `bounds` to `{min:[-5,0,-3], max:[4,5,4]}`. Compute the bounds center componentwise as `(min+max)/2`, set `origin` to its negation, and copy that vector to `modelRoot.position`; thus origin is `[0.5,-2.5,-0.5]`. Keep mesh local positions unchanged. Use a PerspectiveCamera with vertical FOV 40 degrees and aspect 4/3. Let `diagonal` be the bounds diagonal length, set `camera.far = diagonal*5` and `camera.near = camera.far/1000`. The initial camera position is `[12,9,14]`, looking at `[0,0,0]` with up `[0,1,0]`.

3. Implement `pick(ndcX,ndcY)` with `THREE.Raycaster.setFromCamera` using the current camera. Consider only meshes whose own `visible` property is true, choose the nearest intersection, set `selectedId` to its ID, and return that ID. Return null and clear selection when nothing is hit. Give only the selected mesh a green emissive highlight (`0x00ff00`, intensity 1); restore every unselected mesh to emissive black with intensity 0. Canvas pointer clicks must convert the canvas-relative position to NDC and call this same function.

4. Implement `setTypeVisible(type,visible)` by changing every mesh of exactly that IFC type and returning true. For an unknown type, return false without changing visibility or selection. Hiding the type containing the selected mesh must clear its selection and highlight. Initially all types are visible.

5. Implement `focusSelected()` to return false when nothing is selected. Otherwise compute the selected mesh’s world-axis-aligned bounding-box center as `target`, set `distance = (diagonal/2)/Math.tan(20*Math.PI/180)*1.25`, place the camera at `target + normalize([1,0.75,1])*distance`, make it look at `target`, update its matrices, and return true. Do not alter FOV, near, far, model transforms, or visibility.

6. Provide Focus and Reset buttons. `reset()` must restore the initial camera pose and clipping values, all-type visibility, origin, mesh transforms and materials, and clear selection. `getState()` must return selectedId, origin, camera position and current look target, near, far, and a visibleTypes map for the four fixture types.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 91.2/100

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Build a standalone Three.js browser app called “Intro Stage Lab.” Use only locally available Three.js code and procedural geometry/materials; load no models, textures, fonts, animation libraries, or remote assets. Use Y-up world units and numeric tolerance 1e-5. Expose `window.introLab` with `scene`, `camera`, `renderer`, `stageRoot`, `emblem`, `titleBars`, `beacons`, `setFrame(f)`, `focusSelected(id)`, `projectBeacon(id)`, `pickAt(clientX, clientY)`, `reset()`, and `getState()`.

1. Render a full-window scene with background `0x101018`, a `PerspectiveCamera(50, aspect, 0.1, 200)`, ambient light, and directional light. Initially place the camera at `(0,6,14)` looking at `(0,0,0)`. Add a 20×0.2×16 ground box centered at `(0,-0.1,0)`. Create `emblem` as a group at `(-3,-4,-20)` containing a torus with radius 1, tube 0.25, and 32 radial segments. Create `titleBars` at `(3,0,0)` containing three separate 3×0.35×0.5 boxes centered at local Y values 0, 0.7, and 1.4.

2. Add exactly three pickable beacon meshes to `beacons`, each a 0.8-radius, 1.5-high cylinder: `left` at `(-6,0.75,2)`, `core` at `(0,0.75,-4)`, and `right` at `(6,0.75,2)`. Give every beacon a unique non-black material color. Canvas pointer clicks must raycast only these beacons, select the first hit, and call `focusSelected` with its ID. Selection must set that beacon’s material emissive color to `0xffffff` and emissive intensity to 0.35 while clearing this highlight on the others.

3. Implement a 30-fps timeline from frame 0 through 120. `setFrame(f)` must round finite input to the nearest integer, clamp it to `[0,120]`, update the scene immediately, and return the resulting frame; non-finite or non-numeric input returns the current frame without changing anything. Define `a=min(frame/15,1)` and `b=min(frame/30,1)`. Set `emblem.position` to `(-3,-4+4*a,-20+20*b)` and uniformly scale `titleBars` by `1+0.25*a`. In `tour` mode, set camera position to `(0,6,14-7*min(frame/60,1))` and look at the origin. Rendering may continue, but time must not advance the frame automatically.

4. `focusSelected(id)` accepts only `left`, `core`, or `right`; for a valid ID it selects that beacon, changes mode to `focus`, positions the camera at beacon position plus `(0,3,5)`, and looks at the beacon center. It returns `true`. An invalid ID returns `false` and changes nothing. `projectBeacon(id)` returns `{x,y,z}` containing that beacon center in current-camera normalized device coordinates, or `null` for an invalid ID. `pickAt(clientX,clientY)` performs the same picking as a canvas click using the renderer canvas bounding rectangle and returns the selected ID or `null`.

5. Provide Frame, Mode, and Selected text readouts plus Step and Reset buttons. Step calls `setFrame(currentFrame+1)`. `reset()` restores frame 0, `tour` mode, no selection, all default highlights, the initial camera, and all timeline transforms. `getState()` returns `{frame,mode,selectedId,cameraPosition,cameraTarget,emblemPosition,titleScale}` with numeric arrays for positions and a numeric scale. Resizing updates camera aspect and renderer size without altering frame, mode, selection, or camera target.

12. deterministic-gizmo-relay-fabrication-lab

打开实际网页 ↗

创建题 · worker timed_out · 历史运行 PASS · 质量分 100.0/100

验收项 7 · 场景 4 · 必做 5 · 复杂度候选分位 0.943

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Build a standalone Three.js browser app called “Gizmo Relay Lab.” Use only local starter dependencies, OrbitControls, procedural geometry, and solid-color materials; make no runtime network requests and load no models, textures, fonts, or animation libraries. Use meters, Y-up coordinates, and radians. Expose `window.relayLab` with `scene`, `camera`, `renderer`, `controls`, `pods`, `dock`, `selectedId`, `time`, and the methods below.

1. Create a perspective camera at `(12,10,16)` looking at `(0,0,0)`, OrbitControls with damping disabled, ambient and directional lighting, and a 20-by-20 grid. Add a `dock` Group at `(0,2,-3)` with rotation `(0,Math.PI/2,0)`. Create three pod Groups named `alpha`, `beta`, and `gamma`, initially at `(-4,0.5,0)`, `(0,0.5,0)`, and `(4,0.5,0)`. Each contains a selectable box mesh of dimensions `(2,1,1)` centered at its Group origin and a child cylinder mast of radius `0.15`, height `1`, centered at local `(0,1,0)`. Give the boxes distinct solid colors.

2. Clicking any pod mesh or mast must raycast through descendants, select its ancestor pod, update a text readout to its ID, and place a visible three-axis translation gizmo at that pod’s world position. Implement pointer dragging along one gizmo axis. Also expose `selectPod(id)` and `dragPod(id,axis,delta)`; both interaction paths must use the same transform rule. Dragging changes only the named local position component by `delta`, clamping x to `[-6,6]`, y to `[0,4]`, and z to `[-4,4]`. An unknown ID, an axis other than `x`, `y`, or `z`, or a non-finite delta returns `false` and changes nothing; valid calls return `true`.

3. Expose `dockPod(id)` and `undockPod(id)`. Docking reparents that pod to `dock`, and undocking reparents it directly to `scene`; both must preserve the pod’s world position and world quaternion, using world-transform-preserving attachment rather than copying local coordinates. The gizmo must remain at the selected pod after either operation. Invalid IDs return `false` without mutation.

4. Expose `focusSelected()`. It must immediately set `controls.target` to the selected pod’s world position `p`, set the camera position to `p+(6,4,6)`, and update controls without tweening. Add a Focus button that invokes this method.

5. Expose `seek(t)`, clamping finite input to `[0,4]` and storing it as `time`. At time `t`, set `dock.rotation.y = Math.PI/2 + (Math.PI/2)*(t/4)` and every mast’s `scale.y = 1 + 0.5*Math.sin(Math.PI*t/4)`; other mast scale components remain `1`. Play/Pause may animate continuously, but `seek` must produce these exact poses without depending on frame rate. Non-finite input leaves state unchanged and returns `false`.

6. Provide Dock, Undock, Reset, and Play/Pause controls plus selected-ID and time readouts. `reset()` must stop playback, restore all initial pod parents, positions, rotations and scales, restore the dock transform, set every mast scale to `(1,1,1)`, select `alpha`, set time to `0`, and restore the initial camera and controls target. All numeric comparisons use tolerance `1e-6`.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 100.0/100

Worker 超时、网页运行状态和质量分是独立记录。此处保留原始 VLM 评分；预览部署不改变评分。

13. persistent-dual-surface-plaque-configurator

打开实际网页 ↗

创建题 · worker completed · 历史运行 PASS · 质量分 80.0/100

验收项 6 · 场景 4 · 必做 4 · 复杂度候选分位 0.932

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Build a standalone Three.js browser app called “Plaque Studio.” Use only local starter dependencies, procedural geometry, solid-color materials, and locally generated canvas textures; load no models, images, font files, or remote assets and make no runtime network requests. Expose `window.plaqueStudio` with `scene`, `camera`, `controls`, `panel`, `frontLabel`, `backLabel`, `getState()`, `setSide(side, patch)`, `setBaseColor(hex)`, `selectSide(side)`, `pickAt(clientX, clientY)`, `save()`, `load()`, and `reset()`.

1. Use Y-up world units. Create a full-window canvas, resize handling, OrbitControls with damping, ambient and directional lighting, and a perspective camera with FOV 45, near 0.1, far 100, initially at (6,4,8) looking at (0,1.2,0). Add a 12×12 floor whose top is y=0. Add a box mesh named `panel`, dimensions 4×2.4×0.3, centered at (0,1.4,0); its front faces +Z.

2. Each label is a 1.8×0.6 plane in a group parented to `panel`. `frontLabel` is at local (0,0,0.151) with zero rotation; `backLabel` is at local (0,0,-0.151) with Y rotation π. Display each label’s text using a generated canvas texture. Default state is `{version:1,baseColor:"#4477aa",selected:"front",front:{text:"HELLO",color:"#ffffff",x:0,y:0,rotation:0,scale:1,visible:true},back:{text:"BACK",color:"#ffcc00",x:0,y:0,rotation:0,scale:1,visible:false}}`. Position x/y, Z rotation in degrees, and uniform scale apply to the corresponding label group; visibility controls both the group and its pickability. Base color updates the panel material immediately.

3. `setSide` accepts only `front` or `back` and merges a valid patch into that side. Text must be a string of at most 24 characters; x must be in [-1,1], y in [-0.7,0.7], rotation in [-180,180], scale in [0.5,2], visible must be boolean, and colors must match `#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}`. Reject unknown keys, unknown sides, non-finite numbers, and invalid values: return false without changing state, scene, or storage. Valid calls return true and update the scene immediately. `setBaseColor` follows the same color rule. `selectSide` changes selection only for a valid side. `getState` returns a deep copy.

4. Use localStorage key `plaqueStudio.v1`. `save()` writes the complete current state as JSON and returns true. `load()` validates every field and default-required key, then applies the state and updates all geometry, materials, textures, and visibility, returning true. Malformed JSON, a wrong version, missing keys, or invalid values make `load()` return false while leaving current state and scene unchanged. On startup, automatically load a valid saved state; otherwise use defaults and remove the invalid stored item. Successful edits do not save automatically.

5. `pickAt` raycasts from canvas-relative client coordinates against visible labels only. A hit selects and returns that side (`front` or `back`); a miss returns null and leaves selection unchanged. Provide DOM controls for text, color, x, y, rotation, scale, visibility, side selection, Save, Load, and Reset.

6. `reset()` restores every default state value, rebuilds both label textures, restores the initial camera position and controls target, removes `plaqueStudio.v1`, and returns a deep copy of the default state without reloading the page. Numeric comparisons use tolerance 1e-5.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 80.0/100

14. deterministic-polar-harbor-constraint-lab

打开实际网页 ↗

创建题 · worker completed · 历史运行 PASS · 质量分 75.0/100

验收项 6 · 场景 4 · 必做 4 · 复杂度候选分位 0.881

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Build a standalone Three.js browser page called “Polar Harbor Lab.” Use only locally available Three.js code, procedural geometry, and solid-color materials; load no models, textures, fonts, data files, or remote assets and make no runtime network requests. Use XY as the play plane, +Z toward the camera, radians, and world units. Numeric comparisons use tolerance 1e-5.

1. Create a full-window canvas, a PerspectiveCamera(75, aspect, 1, 200) at (0,0,100) looking at the origin, ambient and directional lighting, and a 500×500 water plane at z=-4. Add an island cylinder of radius 8 and height 4 centered at (0,0,-2), a 64-segment circular track of radius 50 in z=0, and a procedural ship group named `ship` made from boxes/cones. The ship remains at radius 50 and z=0. Its position is `(50*cos(angle),50*sin(angle),0)` and its rotation.z is `angle` when direction is +1 or `angle-Math.PI` when direction is -1. Initial angle is Math.PI/2 and direction is +1.

2. Create 31 gold coins in a group named `coins`, using CylinderGeometry(2,2,0.5,16) rotated x=Math.PI/2. For fixture index i=0…30, set angle=`Math.PI/2+(i+1)*2*Math.PI/32`, position=`(50*cos(angle),50*sin(angle),0)`, and name=`coin-${i}`. Collected coins remain children but become invisible. Use dark SphereGeometry(2,16,16) cannonballs in a group named `cannonballs`.

3. Expose `window.harborLab` with `scene`, `camera`, `ship`, `coins`, `cannonballs`, `step(ms)`, `toggleDirection()`, `spawnBall(angle,radius)`, `reset()`, and `getState()`. Do not advance simulation from requestAnimationFrame; rendering may continue, but motion occurs only through `step`. `step(ms)` accepts only integers from 0 through 10000; otherwise throw RangeError without changing state. Advance in exact 1 ms ticks: increase time, move angle by `direction*0.0014`, increase every existing ball radius by 0.035, then spawn a ball at radius 0 whenever time is divisible by 750. Automatic spawn angles cycle through 0, Math.PI, Math.PI/2, 3*Math.PI/2. Ball position is `(radius*cos(angle),radius*sin(angle),0)`.

4. After each tick, collect every visible coin whose ship-to-coin Euclidean distance is <=6, adding one score point per coin. A ball destroys the ship when its distance to the ship is <=6. Set status to `destroyed` on a hit, or `won` when all coins are collected; once either terminal status is reached, later ticks and steps change nothing. `spawnBall` accepts finite angles and radii from 0 through 50 and returns the created mesh; reject other inputs with RangeError.

5. `toggleDirection()` multiplies direction by -1 and immediately updates ship rotation. `reset()` restores time=0, score=0, angle=Math.PI/2, direction=+1, status=`running`, all coins visible, no balls, and the initial ship transform. `getState()` returns a fresh plain object containing time, score, angle, direction, status, collectedCount, and ballCount. Add buttons for Step 1 ms, Step 100 ms, Toggle, and Reset plus a text readout mirroring `getState()`; API calls and buttons must produce identical updates.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 75.0/100

Worker 超时、网页运行状态和质量分是独立记录。此处保留原始 VLM 评分；预览部署不改变评分。

维度分数理由
instruction3/4All primary requirements and task numeric fixtures were observed working. A minor edge-case collision gap remains: a ball spawned essentially at the ship center did not destroy it, though the specified collision scenario passed.
interaction3/4Step, Toggle, Reset, repeated sequences, keyboard activation, terminal freeze and recovery all work with clear state feedback. The mobile panel obscures the main 3D feedback, and collision feedback is not consistent for every ball position.
spatial3/4The scene's track, coin ring, island and ship are correctly positioned in XY, and depth/occlusion are coherent. The very small floor-plane ship, thick flat coins, and hidden mobile ship limit spatial clarity in key views.
stability3/4Desktop reload, repeated operations, terminal state and keyboard use were stable; the page loaded without console errors and made no secondary network requests. The mobile viewport causes a major scene-visibility problem rather than a crash.
visual3/4The dark harbor palette, cyan track, gold coins and compact technical readout form a coherent visual hierarchy on desktop. Mobile composition is significantly compromised by the panel overlap.

15. persistent-character-patrol-route-studio

创建题 · worker completed · 历史运行 PASS · 质量分 97.5/100

验收项 7 · 场景 4 · 必做 5 · 复杂度候选分位 0.977

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Build a standalone Three.js browser app called “Patrol Route Studio.” Use only local starter dependencies and procedural geometry; make no runtime network requests. Use meters, Y-up coordinates, character-forward local +Z, fixed steps dt=1/60 second, and numeric tolerance 1e-5. Expose `window.patrolStudio` with `scene`, `camera`, `renderer`, `controls`, `character`, `routeLine`, `leftLeg`, `rightLeg`, `getState()`, `setPlaying(flag)`, `setMode(mode)`, `step(n)`, `setWaypoint(i,x,z)`, `save()`, `load()`, `importState(text)`, and `reset()`.

1. Create a responsive scene with ambient and directional lighting, OrbitControls, and a PerspectiveCamera(50,aspect,0.1,100) initially at (8,7,10), looking at (0,0,0). Add a 16×12 ground plane at y=0. Build `character` as a Group containing a 0.6×0.8×0.35 body centered at local y=1.1 and two 0.18×0.7×0.18 legs pivoted at local (±0.18,0.7,0), with geometry extending downward. The default route is A=(-3,0), B=(3,0), C=(3,4), displayed by a continuously updated line at y=0.03 and three waypoint markers.

2. Initial state is position A, yaw 0, targetIndex 1, steps 0, distance 0, mode `walk`, playing false, and locomotion state `idle`. Modes are `walk` at 2 m/s and `run` at 4 m/s. While playing, each `step(1)` moves toward the current target by min(modeSpeed/60,remainingDistance), sets yaw to atan2(dx,dz) for a nonzero horizontal target delta, and adds actual travel to distance. On exact arrival, increment targetIndex modulo route length; discard unused movement and advance at most one waypoint per step. `step(n)` performs exactly n fixed steps regardless of rendering, and pausing must not change position, targetIndex, steps, or distance.

3. While playing, locomotion state equals mode; otherwise it is `idle`. Define phase=2π*distance/1.2. While playing set leftLeg.rotation.x=0.6*sin(phase) and rightLeg.rotation.x=-0.6*sin(phase); while paused set both to zero. Update the character's world transform and route display after every API operation.

4. `setWaypoint` accepts integer indices 0–2 and finite coordinates within x=[-8,8], z=[-6,6]. It returns true and preserves playback state on success. Out-of-range indices, nonfinite coordinates, boundary violations, or routes that would make any consecutive segment (including C-to-A) shorter than 0.5 return false without mutation and set `lastError` to a nonempty string.

5. `getState()` returns a JSON-safe object containing version 1, route, mode, playing, steps, position, yaw, targetIndex, distance, locomotion, and lastError. `save()` stores that object in localStorage key `patrol-route-studio-v1` and returns its JSON text. `load()` and `importState(text)` validate the same fields, types, route constraints, targetIndex range 0–2, nonnegative integer steps, and nonnegative finite distance; valid input restores the complete simulation and returns true. Missing saves, malformed JSON, or invalid values return false without changing the current scene or state.

6. Provide Play/Pause, Walk/Run, Save, Load, and Reset controls wired to the API. `reset()` restores every initial value and the default route but does not delete the saved slot. Clear `lastError` after every successful API operation.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 97.5/100

修改题 · worker completed · 历史运行 PASS · 质量分 97.5/100

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Extend the existing browser client with an offline **Animation Lab** while preserving the current Play, Settings, Quit, and multiplayer join behavior.

1. Add an `Animation Lab` button to the main menu. Activating it must hide the menu and open a new local module without calling `joinGameUrl`, creating a connector, or making any network request. Provide a `Back to menu` control that disposes the lab renderer, cancels its animation frame, removes lab DOM, and restores the original menu. Use the repository's local Three.js import map and procedural geometry only.

2. Build a Y-up scene with a ground grid on XZ and a perspective camera at `(6,4,8)` looking at `(0,1,0)`. Create a hierarchy named `actor`, `torso`, `head`, `upperArmL`, `upperArmR`, and `ball`; also store each name in `userData.nodeId`. `actor` starts at `(0,0,0)`. Its children are: a `0.8 x 1.0 x 0.4` torso box centered at `(0,1.2,0)`; a radius-`0.3` head sphere at `(0,2,0)`; arm pivot groups at `(-0.55,1.55,0)` and `(0.55,1.55,0)`, each containing a `0.2 x 0.8 x 0.2` box centered at local `(0,-0.4,0)`; and a radius-`0.25` ball at `(0,1.1,0.55)`. Expose the scene, camera, actor, and a `sampleClip(time)` function on `window.animationLab` for inspection.

3. Define one inline clip with duration `4` seconds and linear interpolation between these keys: `actor.position.y` is `0, 0.25, 0, 0.25, 0`; `upperArmL.rotation.x` in degrees is `0, -60, 0, 60, 0`; and `upperArmR.rotation.x` is `0, 60, 0, -60, 0`, at times `0, 1, 2, 3, 4` respectively. Convert degrees to radians only when assigning rotations. Between keys use `value = a + (b-a)*(time-ta)/(tb-ta)`. Sampling must change only these three channels, leaving all other local transforms unchanged. Thus at `t=1.5`, root Y is `0.125`, left X rotation is `-30` degrees, and right X rotation is `30` degrees.

4. Provide Play/Pause, Reset, Step, a speed selector with `0.5`, `1`, and `2`, a numeric time input, and Set Time. Start paused at `t=0` with speed `1`. During playback accumulate at most `0.25` seconds of real frame delta, advance in fixed `1/120`-second simulation steps multiplied by speed, and wrap playback time modulo `4`. Pause must prevent time changes. Step is allowed only while paused and advances exactly `1/120` seconds, wrapping modulo `4`. Reset pauses, sets time to `0` and speed to `1`, samples the clip, and clears lab output/error text without changing saved game settings. Display time to three decimals.

5. Set Time accepts finite numeric values from `0` through `4`, inclusive, pauses playback, and samples that exact time; paused `t=4` must show the endpoint rather than wrapping. Empty, nonnumeric, NaN, infinite, or out-of-range input must leave time, pose, speed, and play state unchanged and display `Invalid time` in a dedicated status element.

6. Add `Export samples`, which writes JSON into a lab textarea with schema `{"duration":4,"sampleRate":30,"samples":[...]}`. Generate exactly `121` samples for `i=0..120`, each `{ "time": i/30, "actorY": ..., "leftArmXDeg": ..., "rightArmXDeg": ... }`, sampling independently without changing current playback state or time. Round every exported number to six decimal places. The exported data must reproduce the linear keys and include the unwrapped endpoint at time `4`.

17. add-a-deterministic-ray-picking-fixture-to-the-model-viewer

修改题 · worker completed · 历史运行 PASS · 质量分 88.8/100

验收项 7 · 场景 4 · 必做 3 · 复杂度候选分位 0.854 · 原仓库代码文件 66

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Extend the existing viewer without changing its normal model-loading, orbit-control, rendering, or drag-and-drop behavior. Add an opt-in diagnostic mode activated by the URL query `?picking-fixture=1`.

1. In fixture mode, do not load a GLB. Create a `Group` named `picking-fixture` containing three logical pick groups, each with the stated `userData.pickId` and one child `Mesh` using `BoxGeometry` and a `MeshStandardMaterial`: `rear` has dimensions (2,2,2), world position (0,0,-2), and child name `rear-surface`; `front` has dimensions (1,1,1), world position (0,0,1), and child name `front-surface`; `offset` has dimensions (1,1,1), world position (3,0,0), and child name `offset-surface`. Use a `PerspectiveCamera` with FOV 60 degrees, aspect 4/3, near 0.1, far 100, position (0,0,12), looking at the origin. Set the orbit target to the origin and the drawing buffer to 800 by 600 pixels. Existing lighting and ground rendering may remain.

2. Expose `window.pickingProbe` with `pick(ndcX, ndcY)`, `project(id)`, `state()`, and `reset()`. `pick` must raycast recursively against only `picking-fixture`, using the current camera, and choose the nearest intersection. Resolve a hit to the nearest ancestor having `userData.pickId`. On a hit, return `{id, point:[x,y,z], distance, objectName}`, where `objectName` is the intersected mesh's name, set `SceneRenderer.selectedObjects` to exactly that mesh, and set the diagnostic selected ID accordingly. On a valid miss, return null, clear both selections, and leave `lastError` null.

3. `project(id)` must return the logical group's world position transformed by Three.js `Vector3.project(currentCamera)` as `{x,y,z}`, or null for an unknown ID. Primary-button canvas pointer picks must use the same picking routine and convert coordinates with `x=2*(clientX-rect.left)/rect.width-1` and `y=-2*(clientY-rect.top)/rect.height+1`; ignore pointers outside the canvas rectangle.

4. `state()` returns `{selectedId, lastError, pickCount}`. NDC inputs are valid only when both are finite numbers in the inclusive range [-1,1]. Invalid input returns null, sets `lastError` to `invalid-ndc`, and leaves selection and `pickCount` unchanged. Every accepted API or pointer pick, whether hit or miss, increments `pickCount` by one and clears `lastError`; projection does not change diagnostics.

5. When `SceneRenderer.enableObjectSelection` is false, picking returns null and leaves selection and all diagnostics unchanged. `reset()` clears selection and `lastError`, sets `pickCount` to zero, and restores the initial camera pose and orbit target, but does not alter rendering-quality, material, environment, or ground settings.

6. Keep the probe unavailable outside fixture mode, preserve ordinary viewer behavior there, and use no remote assets, network calls, screenshots, or image-based decisions.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 88.8/100

18. add-a-live-building-part-spatial-constraint-audit

打开实际网页 ↗

修改题 · worker completed · 历史运行 PASS · 质量分 70.0/100

验收项 7 · 场景 4 · 必做 5 · 复杂度候选分位 0.917 · 原仓库代码文件 17

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Extend the existing OSMBuilding viewer with a deterministic spatial-constraint audit while preserving its current building geometry, OrbitControls, file loading, and info GUI.

1. Audit mode is enabled only when the URL contains the `audit` query parameter. The evaluation URL is `?type=way&id=100&fromFile=./test/data/spatial-audit.osm&audit`. Loading that URL must use the supplied local XML and make no OSM API request. Without `audit`, do not create audit helpers or change existing behavior.

2. After the initial building meshes have been added, expose `window.osmAudit` with `refresh()` and `getReport()`. `refresh()` must call `scene.updateMatrixWorld(true)`, discard the previous report and helpers, recompute from live meshes, and return the new report. `getReport()` returns the latest report. The report is `{outerId:"100", parts:[...], issues:[]}`, with parts sorted by numeric OSM id. Each part entry is `{id, bounds:{min:[x,y,z],max:[x,y,z]}, footprint:[minX,minZ,maxX,maxZ], baseY, topY, contained, heightViolation}`.

3. For each part, find its wall mesh named `b<id>` and compute bounds with `new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(wall)` in world coordinates. Compute the outer reference identically from `b100`, including it even though it is invisible. Keep this internally computed outer-wall Box3 as `outer.bounds` for the formulas below; `outer.bounds` is not a serialized report field. `baseY` and `topY` are the part bounds minimum and maximum Y. With epsilon `0.001` in every comparison, `contained` is true exactly when all four part footprint limits lie within the outer footprint, allowing epsilon at each boundary. `heightViolation` is `topY > outer.bounds.max[1] + 0.001`. Do not use roof meshes, helper objects, visibility, or raw latitude/longitude for these calculations.

4. Add a scene group named `spatialAudit`. On every refresh, remove and dispose its previous children, then add one `Box3Helper` for the outer wall and one per valid part. Use blue `0x0000ff` for the outer box, green `0x00ff00` for a part that is contained and has no height violation, and red `0xff0000` otherwise. Each part helper must use the same Box3 instance used to populate its report entry's bounds arrays, and the outer helper must use the internally computed outer-wall Box3. Helpers must not alter building meshes, materials, camera, or controls.

5. The supplied fixture has outer way 100 and parts 201 and 202. Part 201 is inside the outer footprint and below its top; part 202 crosses the outer footprint and is taller than the outer wall. Therefore the initial report must classify 201 as contained with no height violation and 202 as neither contained nor height-compliant. Recomputing after a mesh transform or geometry replacement must use the new live bounds, not cached XML dimensions.

6. If an expected outer or part wall is missing or produces an empty Box3, append a human-readable string containing its id to `issues`, omit an invalid part entry and its helper, and continue auditing other parts. If the outer reference is invalid, set every valid part's `contained` and `heightViolation` to false. Refresh resets only audit state; it must not reload XML, rebuild the building, or reset user camera/control state.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 70.0/100

Worker 超时、网页运行状态和质量分是独立记录。此处保留原始 VLM 评分；预览部署不改变评分。

interaction2/4OrbitControls work and refresh is repeatable through the exposed API, but the audit has no page UI. Required visible feedback beyond the colored overlays is implicit, and recovery/invalid-state sequences could not be exercised.
spatial3/4The building volume, wall geometry, perspective, occlusion, and spatially separated green/red/blue constraint boxes are coherent across three views. Small viewport framing can make the model tiny in empty space, and fresh-bounds behavior after transformation remains unverified.
stability3/4Initial load, reload, refresh, orbit, and desktop/mobile layouts were reliable. Resource diagnostics were clean apart from favicon 404, and there were no OSM API requests. Unverified transform, missing-wall, and helper-disposal recovery flows preclude a higher rating.
visual3/4The scene is deliberately minimal but coherent: a neutral gray building, dark background, perspective grid, and strongly differentiated outline colors. The subject is readable, though large empty framing reduces focus.

19. add-persistent-lighting-workspaces-with-atomic-3d-restoration

打开实际网页 ↗

修改题 · worker timed_out · 历史运行 PASS · 质量分 41.2/100

验收项 6 · 场景 4 · 必做 3 · 复杂度候选分位 0.784 · 原仓库代码文件 43

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Extend the existing editor with named lighting workspaces while preserving all current light painting, camera switching, outliner, and preview behavior.

1. Add a “Workspaces” section to the Outliner containing a name input, a “Save workspace” button, and a row for each saved workspace with “Load” and “Delete” buttons. Saving creates a deep, independent snapshot of the complete ordered lights and cameras arrays, including every light field, every camera field, and all selection flags. Each record must have `{version:1,id,name,createdAt,lights,cameras}`, with a unique string id and a millisecond timestamp. Persist the array immediately under the localStorage key `env.workspaces.v1`; do not replace or disable the existing light/camera persistence.

2. Trim workspace names before saving. Names must contain 1–40 characters and must be unique case-insensitively. For an empty, overlong, or duplicate name, show a toast explaining the problem and leave both the scene and stored workspace array unchanged. Saving and loading must not reload the page.

3. Loading a workspace must atomically replace the current lights and cameras with deep clones of that snapshot, preserving order and selection. The selected restored camera must become the default camera with its saved position and rotation, and every restored light must immediately resume its saved position, target, and appearance. Loading changes only light/camera scene state: it must not change the active panel, light-painting mode, or saved workspace list.

4. On startup, read `env.workspaces.v1` without network access. Malformed JSON or a non-array value produces an empty workspace list without throwing and without modifying the current scene. Ignore array entries that are not objects or lack `version`, `id`, `name`, `lights`, or `cameras`. Deleting a workspace removes only that record, persists the remaining records, and leaves the current scene unchanged.

5. Use this deterministic verification case: start with one existing Softbox light and one selected camera; set the light’s `latlon` to `{x:0,y:0}` and `target` to `{x:1,y:2,z:3}`, and the camera position to `[4,5,6]` and rotation to `[0.1,0.2,0.3]` radians. Save it as `Studio A`, then change the light to `latlon={x:1,y:1}`, `target={x:0,y:0,z:0}` and the camera position to `[0,0,0]`. Loading `Studio A` must restore every original value.

6. For spatial verification, use the existing radius-3 light rig and mappings `phi=(1-latlon.y)*PI/2`, `theta=-PI/2-PI*latlon.x`, and `position=(3*sin(phi)*sin(theta),3*cos(phi),3*sin(phi)*cos(theta))`. Thus the verification light’s restored world position is `(-3,0,0)` within floating-point tolerance; changing its target must not alter that position.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 41.2/100

20. add-a-deterministic-guided-gaze-target-tour

打开实际网页 ↗

修改题 · worker timed_out · 历史运行 未记录 · 质量分 待评测 / 无质量分

验收项 7 · 场景 4 · 必做 3 · 复杂度候选分位 0.799 · 原仓库代码文件 31

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Extend the existing test demo with a deterministic, world-space gaze-target tour. Keep the current avatar, idle mixer, blinking, audio/S-key speech, OrbitControls, and Spacebar look toggle working.

1. After the glTF load, add a sphere named `gazeTarget` directly to `app.scene`, using `SphereGeometry(0.15, 16, 8)` and any opaque material. Add a closed `LineLoop` named `gazePath` through these world-space waypoints in order: p0=(2,2,4), p1=(-2,2,4), p2=(-2,2,-4), p3=(2,2,-4). These helpers use scene/world coordinates, not avatar-local coordinates. Do not load new assets.

2. The tour duration is 12 seconds with four 3-second linear segments. For finite time t, compute u=((t % 12)+12)%12, i=floor(u/3), f=(u-3*i)/3, and set the target to lerp(p[i],p[(i+1)%4],f). Thus t=0 gives p0 and negative times wrap. The tour initially runs automatically. P toggles only tour running/paused state. R sets tour time to 0 and resumes the tour; it must not change look-enabled state, camera, avatar transforms, audio, blink state, or mixer state.

3. While the existing look mode is enabled, call `headLook` with `gazeTarget.position` instead of `app.camera.position`. Space must retain the existing enable/blend-back behavior. Pausing the tour freezes only the target; it does not disable gaze tracking. When look mode is disabled, the target may continue touring, but manual look stepping must not rotate the head, eyes, or avatar root.

4. Expose `window.actorDemo` with `target`, `setManualMode(boolean)`, `seekTour(t)`, `step(dt)`, `stepLook(dt)`, `getState()`, and `gazeError()`. Manual mode starts false. When true, requestAnimationFrame must supply zero dt to both tour advancement and the look wrapper, leaving unrelated baseline ticks untouched. `seekTour` updates only normalized tour time and target position. `step(dt)` advances the tour and invokes the active look wrapper once with dt; `stepLook(dt)` invokes it once without advancing time. Both stepping methods require manual mode and finite dt>=0; otherwise throw `RangeError`. `seekTour` throws `RangeError` for non-finite input. `getState()` returns `{tourTime, running, manual, looking, targetPosition:[x,y,z]}`.

5. Define `gazeError()` as the angle in radians between the effector's world-transformed local +Z axis and the normalized vector from its world position to the target, using acos of the dot product clamped to [-1,1]. With look enabled and manual mode on, `seekTour(0)` followed by 600 calls to `stepLook(1/60)` must produce gazeError()<0.001 radians. A seek alone must not change any head, eye, or avatar-root quaternion.

6. Update the existing info text to document P pause/resume and R tour reset. All geometry, controls, and APIs must work from local source and assets without runtime network access.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 待评测 / 无质量分

21. persist-and-restore-the-mnist-activation-scene-view

打开实际网页 ↗

修改题 · worker timed_out · 历史运行 FAIL · 质量分 待评测 / 无质量分

验收项 6 · 场景 4 · 必做 3 · 复杂度候选分位 0.868 · 原仓库代码文件 361

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Modify the existing MNIST CNN demo in `demos/src/components/models/MnistCnn.vue` without changing its drawing, prediction, intermediate-canvas, or OrbitControls workflows except as specified below. All functionality must be testable without downloading model weights or fonts.

1. Persist the 3D view in localStorage under `kerasjs.mnist-cnn.viz.v1` as JSON with shape `{version:1,camera:{position:[x,y,z],target:[x,y,z]},hiddenLayers:string[],labelsRotating:boolean}`. Defaults are position `[0,0,5000]`, target `[0,0,0]`, no hidden layers, and `labelsRotating:true`. Save after orbit-control changes and every view-setting change. Restore after WebGL initialization and before animation begins. Store hidden layer names uniquely in `LAYER_DISPLAY_CONFIG` declaration order.

2. Add per-layer visibility controls for the existing layer names and a labels-rotation toggle. Hidden layers must set their corresponding 3D points, lines, and text containers invisible rather than deleting them. The view state must survive a full page reload, and newly rebuilt activation geometry after a later prediction must immediately use the restored camera and visibility settings.

3. Change CLEAR so it removes only generated activation points, lines, and text. It must retain the renderer, camera, OrbitControls, highlight box, and persisted view state. CLEAR retains its existing input/output/stroke cleanup. A separate `RESET VIEW` control must restore all view defaults, make every layer visible, enable label rotation, and save those defaults without clearing the digit, prediction output, or activation geometry.

4. Expose `window.__mnistViz` after initialization with `loadFixture()`, `getSnapshot()`, `setCamera(position,target)`, `setLayerVisible(name,visible)`, `setLabelsRotating(value)`, and `resetView()`. `loadFixture()` replaces previous fixture geometry with three `THREE.Points` objects named `layer:input`, `layer:activation_1`, and `layer:dense_2`. Each has nine vertices: x and y independently take `[-10,0,10]` in row-major order, while every z is respectively `-155`, `-105`, or `285`. Fixture creation must apply current visibility and require no model inference. `getSnapshot()` returns a deep-copied normalized state, current camera/target arrays, each fixture object's name, visibility and flat position array, label-pivot y rotations, and `highlightBoxPresent`. The setters update the live scene and persistence; unknown layer names or non-boolean visibility/rotation values return `false` without changing state, otherwise they return `true`.

5. Validate loaded persistence defensively. Malformed JSON, a non-object root, or wrong `version` uses all defaults. Invalid camera arrays fall back individually to their defaults; a valid array contains exactly three finite numbers, and a position equal to the target is invalid. Filter hidden names to known unique layer names. A non-boolean `labelsRotating` uses `true`. Ignore unknown properties, and localStorage read/write failures must not prevent rendering.

6. Preserve the existing animation rule exactly: while enabled, every label pivot's `rotation.y` increases by `0.015` per animation frame; while disabled, those rotations remain unchanged. Numeric checks use absolute tolerance `1e-5`.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 待评测 / 无质量分
