22. add-deterministic-commit-picking-and-camera-focus

打开实际网页 ↗

修改题 · worker completed · 历史运行 PASS · 质量分 18.8/100

验收项 7 · 场景 4 · 必做 4 · 复杂度候选分位 0.926 · 原仓库代码文件 41

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Extend the existing repo-surf browser app with commit picking and a deterministic offline inspection mode. Preserve the existing branch geometry, commit pooling, playback, and normal repository-loading flow except where specified.

1. Add a fixture mode activated by the exact query string `?fixture=picking`. It must bypass repository fetching and the loading screen, make no network requests, and render one linear branch containing five commits in newest-first order. Their full SHAs are 40 repetitions of `a`, `b`, `c`, `d`, and `e`, respectively; their messages are `commit A` through `commit E`. Each commit except the oldest has the next SHA as its only parent, and the oldest has no parents. Use the existing layout code and configuration for their world positions.

2. Make rendered commit objects pointer-pickable. Set `userData.commitSha` to the full SHA on each Commit root. Clicking a visible commit must select exactly that commit, using the nearest intersection when hits overlap. Show a DOM panel with `data-testid="commit-inspector"` and child fields `data-testid="inspector-sha"`, `inspector-message`, and `inspector-index`; display the full SHA, complete message, and zero-based commit index. Hovering a pickable commit must set the canvas cursor to `pointer`, and leaving it must restore the previous cursor.

3. Expose selection spatially: the selected Commit root's local scale must be `[1.5, 1.5, 1.5]`, while every unselected root remains `[1, 1, 1]`. On selection, immediately move the camera to world position `P + (0, 6, -10)`, where `P` is the selected commit's world position, set `camera.up` to `(0, 1, 0)`, and look directly at `P`. While a commit remains selected, CameraOperator must not overwrite these camera values, although track playback may continue.

4. Pressing Escape, clicking empty canvas space, or calling the reset API below must clear the selection and hover state, remove the inspector, restore all commit scales to 1, and hand camera control back to the existing CameraOperator. It must not reload the page, reset the commit fixture, or restart the playback clock.

5. In fixture mode only, expose `window.__repoSurfPicking` after the scene is ready. `getState()` must return `{selectedSha, commits, camera}`, where commits contain `{sha,message,index,position}` in newest-first order and camera contains numeric `position`, `target`, and `up` triples. `projectCommit(sha)` must return that commit's current world position projected with the live camera as `{x,y,visible}`, with x/y in normalized device coordinates and visible indicating NDC bounds `[-1,1]` on all axes. `reset()` performs requirement 4. Unknown SHAs must return null without changing state. Do not expose this API outside fixture mode.

6. Keep ordinary URLs on the existing fetch/loading experience. All fixture behavior must work from local source and bundled assets without external services, and no requirement depends on screenshot comparison.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 18.8/100

23. add-animated-whole-cube-inspection-rotations

打开实际网页 ↗

修改题 · worker completed · 历史运行 PASS · 质量分 97.5/100

验收项 7 · 场景 4 · 必做 4 · 复杂度候选分位 0.907 · 原仓库代码文件 46

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Extend the existing browser Rubik's Cube demo with whole-cube visual inspection rotations. These rotations move the rendered cube, not its logical stickers.

1. Preserve all existing face-turn, scramble, solve, fold/unfold, position, speed-slider, orbit, mouse, and touch behavior. Add sidebar buttons with IDs `inspect-x`, `inspect-y`, `inspect-z`, and `inspect-reset`, labeled `Inspect X`, `Inspect Y`, `Inspect Z`, and `Reset inspection`. Add public methods `cube.inspect(axis)` and `cube.resetInspection()`; the buttons call these methods with `x`, `y`, or `z`.

2. Create one `THREE.Group` named `inspectionPivot`, positioned at `(0,0,0)` with identity initial quaternion, and add it to the cube scene. Every normal facet mesh and every temporary split facet mesh must be a descendant of this group whenever displayed. At identity, retain the baseline facet geometry and local coordinates. Do not parent the camera, renderer, or OrbitControls to the group.

3. For a valid idle request, `inspect(axis)` rotates the entire pivot by exactly +90 degrees about the fixed world X, Y, or Z axis through `(0,0,0)`, using the right-hand rule. Compose successive rotations by premultiplying: `targetQuaternion = axisQuaternion * currentQuaternion`. Animate for exactly 250 ms using normalized linear progress and quaternion slerp from the starting quaternion to the target; the speed slider must not affect this duration. Snap to the exact target on completion.

4. Inspection must not call `cubeState.rotate`, alter the Singmaster position string, change `isFolded`, or enqueue face-turn commands. Return `true` when an inspection request starts. Return `false` without changing anything for an invalid axis, while an inspection is running, or while `cube.isActive()` is true. During inspection animation, face turns, folding, scrambling, solving, position setting, and face dragging must remain blocked; they must work again afterward.

5. `resetInspection()` returns `false` while an inspection is running or the cube is active. Otherwise it immediately restores the pivot to identity, without changing logical cube state, and returns `true`. Repeated X/Y/Z requests must compose rather than replace one another.

6. Existing face-turn and fold animations, including split meshes, must remain inside the pivot and therefore inherit the current inspection transform. Update world matrices before rendering or raycasting so orbit viewing and drag picking continue to use correct world-space positions after inspection.

7. Expose `cube.getInspectionQuaternion()` returning a new plain object `{x,y,z,w}`, and `cube.isInspecting()` returning whether inspection animation is active. Use only existing local dependencies and add no network service, asset, persistence, or build-step requirement.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 97.5/100

Worker 超时、网页运行状态和质量分是独立记录。此处保留原始 VLM 评分；预览部署不改变评分。

维度分数理由
instruction4/4All user-facing requirements are delivered: labeled inspect X/Y/Z and reset buttons via public-style API, whole-cube visual rotations that compose and reset, preserved scramble/solve/fold/turn/slider behavior, and keyboard activation. Internal method/quaternion details could not be independently asserted but nothing visible contradicts them.
interaction4/4Primary inspection controls, composition of repeated rotations, reset recovery, face turns, fold/unfold and scramble/solve all worked reliably; hamburger reveal and keyboard Enter are also functional. Minor: sidebar buttons are slightly clipped at the left edge when open.
spatial4/4Cube geometry, depth, occlusion and materials remain coherent across initial and multiple inspected views; rotations are true fixed-world axis rotations of the rendered cube with stickers following the faces, and the unfolded net keeps correct spatial layout.
stability3/4Tested flows are stable on desktop and mobile and reload is clean. Local nonblocking issue: a pre-existing cordova.js 404 in console and the sidebar open/close uses an off-canvas slide that clips button labels on the left until fully settled.
visual4/4Clean centered composition, distinct saturated face colors and consistent face lighting make orientation changes easy to read; the sidebar type is legible at desktop and mobile sizes.

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Extend the existing TetraFlow browser demo with a procedural 3D inspection rig and deterministic playback controls. Preserve the start button, audio initialization, existing SuperFlow and its children, renderer, lighting, resize handling, and OrbitControls limits.

1. After creating SuperFlow, add a `THREE.Group` named `inspectionRig` directly to the scene at position `(0,0,0)`. Add one `THREE.Mesh` named `inspectionRing` using `TorusGeometry(4, 0.02, 8, 96)`, rotated `Math.PI / 2` about X so the ring lies in the XZ plane. Add exactly eight marker meshes named `marker-0` through `marker-7`, each using its own `TetrahedronGeometry(0.3, 0)` and its own material. For zero-based index `i`, set `a = 2 * Math.PI * i / 8` and local position `(4 * cos(a), 0, 4 * sin(a))`. Use default color `0x40c0c0`; do not merge the markers or make them GPU instances.

2. Replace wall-clock animation time with a simulation-time variable initially equal to `0`. While running, advance it by the clock delta on each animation frame; while paused, do not advance it. Continue calling `superFlow.update(simulationTime)` and rendering while paused. Set `inspectionRig.rotation.y = 0.25 * simulationTime`. For each marker, set `rotation.x = 0.5 * simulationTime + i * Math.PI / 4` and `rotation.z = -0.2 * simulationTime`; marker local positions must remain as specified above.

3. Space toggles between running and paused. While paused, ArrowRight advances simulation time by exactly `0.25` seconds and immediately applies the new transforms; ArrowRight while running has no effect. `R` resets simulation time to `0`, enters the paused state, clears selection, restores the initial camera position using `(0, 2.5, 12).setLength(17)`, sets the OrbitControls target to `(0,0,0)`, and updates controls. Ignore modified key events and auto-repeat events, and prevent ArrowRight from scrolling the page.

4. Implement primary-button pointer picking against only the eight marker meshes using `THREE.Raycaster` and the actual canvas bounding rectangle. A marker hit selects the lowest-index intersected marker, changes only that marker to `0xff8000`, and restores every other marker to `0x40c0c0`. A click that hits no marker clears selection. Add a text element with id `flowStatus` whose content is exactly `Selected marker: none` when none is selected or `Selected marker: i` for marker `i`. Selection must not pause animation or alter marker geometry or transforms.

5. Add a concise on-screen help element describing Space, ArrowRight, R, and marker clicking. Keep orbit and zoom functional, including while paused. Do not add remote assets, network requests, external fixtures, or new dependencies.

6. For automated inspection, expose `window.tetraFlowDiagnostics` after initialization with references `scene`, `camera`, `controls`, `superFlow`, `rig`, and `markers`, plus functions `getSimulationTime()`, `isPaused()`, `step()`, and `reset()`. The `step()` and `reset()` functions must follow the same rules as their keyboard equivalents.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 75.0/100

25. add-a-deterministic-offline-feature-lattice-visualization

打开实际网页 ↗

修改题 · worker completed · 历史运行 PASS · 质量分 66.2/100

验收项 6 · 场景 4 · 必做 3 · 复杂度候选分位 0.796 · 原仓库代码文件 41

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Extend the existing demo with an offline, deterministic Three.js visualization without changing the existing track-selection, server-fetch, Mapper, or audio-driven visualization behavior.

1. Add a `Run Fixture` button with ID `fixture-btn` beside the existing GO button. Clicking it must call the existing `destroy()` flow, then start the new visualization directly in `#viz-container`; it must not require a selected track, load audio, instantiate Mapper, or make any network request. Add a `Fixture Lattice` option to the visualization selector only if useful for integration, but the button must always work.

2. Implement a visualization class with `start()`, `setTime(t)`, `play()`, `pause()`, `getState()`, and `destroy()` methods, and expose the active instance as `window.fixtureViz`. Its scene must contain a group named `fixture-root` and nine unit BoxGeometry meshes named `cell-r-c` for rows and columns 0–2. For cell `r,c`, use local position `x=(c-1)*2`, `z=(r-1)*2`, and feature index `j=(r*3+c)%3`. Use a PerspectiveCamera at `(8,8,8)` looking at the origin. Do not load models, textures, or other assets.

3. Embed exactly this feature timeline, with times 0, 1, 2, and 3 seconds: `F0=[0,0.25,0.5]`, `F1=[0.5,0.75,1]`, `F2=[1,0.5,0]`, `F3=[0.2,0.4,0.6]`. At time `t`, linearly interpolate adjacent frames with `a=t-floor(t)`; at `t=3`, use F3. For every cell compute `v` from its feature and set `scale.y=h=0.5+2*v`, `position.y=h/2`, and leave `scale.x=scale.z=1`. Thus every box bottom remains at local y=0. Set `fixture-root.rotation.y=t*Math.PI/4`.

4. Starting or rerunning the fixture must reset time to 0 and render that state. `setTime(t)` must immediately render, clamp finite values below 0 to 0 and above 3 to 3, and return the clamped time. For `NaN`, Infinity, a missing argument, or a non-number, it must throw `TypeError` without changing time or the scene. `getState()` must return `{time, heights, rotationY}`, where `heights` is a nine-element array in row-major cell order.

5. The fixture must not advance autonomously after `start()`. `play()` advances from the current time using elapsed monotonic clock time, stops at 3 seconds, and `pause()` freezes the current time. Repeated `play()` calls while already playing must not create additional animation loops. `destroy()` must cancel animation, remove the renderer canvas, dispose fixture geometry and materials, and clear `window.fixtureViz` if it references that instance.

6. Rerunning via `fixture-btn` must leave exactly one fixture canvas and nine fixture cells. Existing non-fixture visualizations must continue through their original audio and Mapper path, and switching away from the fixture must invoke its cleanup. Keep all fixture data and code local so this mode works with the server unavailable.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 66.2/100

Worker 超时、网页运行状态和质量分是独立记录。此处保留原始 VLM 评分；预览部署不改变评分。

维度分数理由
instruction3/4Core flows work (button without track/network, API, reset, invalid input, play/pause, rerun), but feature sampling omits row index so required heights are wrong and switching away from the fixture skips cleanup.
interaction3/4Run Fixture, rerun, API play/pause and invalid-input recovery are reliable with clear feedback and correct rotation animation; local issues are the missing switch-away cleanup and selector console TypeError.
spatial2/4The lattice is genuine and coherent from the fixed camera, but rows within each column render identical heights at all tested times, contradicting per-cell feature mapping — a major spatial data error revealed by operation.
stability3/4Rerun, reload and invalid inputs are stable, maintaining one canvas/nine cells without crashes. Nonblocking issues: mobile panel clipping, selector TypeError on switch-away, and load-time fetchtracks connection-refused error.
visual3/4Coherent legible desktop composition with strong color grouping and consistent styling; mobile clipping and uniform column heights reduce fidelity.
Common rubric：本批通用检查与评分锚点

26. deterministic-collision-aware-enemy-spawn-planner

打开实际网页 ↗

修改题 · worker completed · 历史运行 PASS · 质量分 23.8/100

验收项 7 · 场景 4 · 必做 3 · 复杂度候选分位 0.785 · 原仓库代码文件 16

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Extend the existing FPS with deterministic, collision-aware enemy spawn placement. Preserve the current scene, weapons, controls, health, kill/respawn flow, and building geometry except for the spawn behavior below. Use local code and procedural geometry only; add no remote assets or runtime network requests.

1. Add a reusable planner and expose `window.spawnLab` with `plan(request)`, `loadFixture()`, `getState()`, and `reset()`. A request has exactly the relevant fields `{count,minDistance,maxDistance,player:[x,z],obstacles:[{id,min:[x,z],max:[x,z]}],occupied:[[x,z]]}`. `plan` must not mutate the scene and returns `{positions,missing}`, where each position is `[x,0.5,z]` and `missing` is the number of requested enemies for which no position was found. Require finite numeric coordinates, integer `count` from 0 through 16, integer distances satisfying `0 <= minDistance <= maxDistance <= 100`, and ordered obstacle bounds. Throw `RangeError` for invalid input without changing lab state.

2. Use an enemy footprint radius of 0.75 and Y-up world coordinates. For each requested enemy, search radii from `maxDistance` down to `minDistance` inclusive in steps of 1. At each radius `r`, test 32 angles in order `j=0…31`, with `a=2πj/32`, `x=player[0]+r*sin(a)`, and `z=player[1]+r*cos(a)`. Restart this complete ordering for every enemy and select the first valid candidate.

3. Reject a candidate when `abs(x)>49.25` or `abs(z)>49.25`. Reject it when it touches or enters an expanded obstacle: `x>=minX-0.75 && x<=maxX+0.75 && z>=minZ-0.75 && z<=maxZ+0.75`. Also reject it when its XZ distance to any occupied point or previously planned position is strictly less than 1.5; exactly 1.5 is allowed. If no candidate survives, increment `missing` and continue with the next requested enemy.

4. `loadFixture()` must suspend normal enemy AI and spawning, then call `plan` with `{count:2,minDistance:10,maxDistance:10,player:[0,0],obstacles:[{id:"block",min:[-1,9],max:[1,11]}],occupied:[]}`. Create a procedural ground plane, a box named `fixture-block` representing those XZ bounds with height 2 and center y=1, and two existing `Enemy` groups named `spawn-fixture-0` and `spawn-fixture-1` at the returned positions. The expected positions are `[1.9509032202,0.5,9.8078528040]` and `[3.8268343237,0.5,9.2387953251]`.

5. `getState()` returns the last request, result, fixture enemy world positions, and whether fixture mode is active. `reset()` removes only fixture enemies, fixture geometry, and lab state, then restores ordinary game updating; it must not reset weapons, health, kills, buildings, or the player camera.

6. Integrate the planner into ordinary `spawnEnemies`: use the camera XZ as player, distances 20 and 40, living enemy XZ positions as occupied points, and world-space XZ bounds of non-destroyed, non-passable meshes in `window.walls` as obstacles. Create only planned positions, preserve initial and on-death spawn counts, and skip positions when the planner reports them missing rather than reverting to random placement.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 23.8/100

visual1/4HUD typography and controls are legible, but the dominant viewport is a flat gray field with a cropped black/brown fragment and overlapping help panel, so scene hierarchy and intended FPS composition are not recognizable.

27. deterministic-hex-picking-and-camera-focus-on-the-debug-page

打开实际网页 ↗

修改题 · worker timed_out · 历史运行 未记录 · 质量分 待评测 / 无质量分

验收项 6 · 场景 4 · 必做 3 · 复杂度候选分位 0.791 · 原仓库代码文件 48

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Extend the existing `/debug` browser page into a deterministic hex-tile picking and camera-focus workbench. No backend, remote asset, image, or random value may be needed.

1. Replace the debug page's random house and single-tile scene contents with one `THREE.InstancedMesh` named `debug-tiles`, using `CylinderGeometry(1, 1, 1, 6)` or the equivalent buffer geometry and flat Lambert material. Preserve the existing lights, renderer, resize behavior, and OrbitControls polar limits. Use this exact fixture, in order: A=(x:0,z:0,height:1,terrain:dirt), B=(1,0,2,stone), C=(0,1,3,sand), D=(-1,1,4,forest), E=(1,1,2,water). For each tile use worldX=((2*x+z)*sqrt(3))/2, worldZ=(3*z)/2, positionY=max(height/2,1), scale=(1,max(height,1),1), and no rotation. Set `mesh.userData.tileIds` to `['A','B','C','D','E']`; instance colors must use the existing terrain palette. Make `/debug` render even when the websocket is unavailable, without changing world-page behavior.

2. Implement left-button picking by raycasting `debug-tiles` and mapping `instanceId` through `tileIds`. Convert pointer coordinates with the renderer canvas's current `getBoundingClientRect()`, not window dimensions. A click is a pointerdown/pointerup pair whose screen-space movement is at most 5 pixels. Clicking a tile selects it; clicking empty space clears selection; drags over 5 pixels and non-left buttons change nothing. Show exact status text `Selected: none` or `Selected: <id>` in an element with `data-testid="selection-status"`.

3. For a selected tile, show a yellow `TorusGeometry(1.15, 0.05, 8, 32)` marker named `selection-marker`, rotated into the XZ plane and positioned at `(worldX, topY+0.05, worldZ)`, where `topY=positionY+max(height,1)/2`. Hide or remove it when nothing is selected. Selection must not alter tile instance matrices.

4. Add a button with `data-testid="focus-selected"`, disabled with no selection. When activated, animate both camera position and OrbitControls target for 300 milliseconds using requestAnimationFrame timestamps, clamped `t=(now-start)/300`, and linear interpolation. The destination target is `(worldX,topY,worldZ)` and destination camera is target plus `(-5,5,0)`. At completion set `focusedId` to the selected id. Selecting another tile, manually starting OrbitControls, or resetting cancels an active focus and clears `focusedId`.

5. Add a `data-testid="reset-debug"` button. Reset cancels animation, clears selection and `focusedId`, restores camera `(-5,5,0)` and target `(0,0,0)`, and leaves the fixture intact. Expose `window.__hexDebug` with `getState()`, `projectTile(id)`, and `reset()`. `getState()` returns selectedId, focusedId, cameraPosition, cameraTarget, markerPosition, and statusText as serializable values; `projectTile(id)` returns the client-pixel coordinates of that tile's top-center projected through the current camera and canvas rectangle.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 待评测 / 无质量分

28. add-exact-surface-queries-and-normal-aligned-terrain-survey-mark

打开实际网页 ↗

修改题 · worker timed_out · 历史运行 PASS · 质量分 60.0/100

验收项 7 · 场景 4 · 必做 4 · 复杂度候选分位 0.839 · 原仓库代码文件 9

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Extend the existing A-Frame terrain project without replacing its procedural terrain, shader, primitive mappings, or existing getElevation behavior.

1. Add a geometry-data method getSurfaceInfo(x, z) beside getElevation. It must query the actual indexed BufferGeometry, not regenerate noise. Project each indexed triangle onto XZ and, for the triangle containing the query point, compute barycentric weights using signed twice-areas: w0=A(P,B,C)/A(A,B,C), w1=A(P,A,C)/A(B,A,C), and w2=1-w0-w1. Accept weights down to -1e-8 and clamp accepted weights to [0,1] before renormalizing. Return {elevation, normal}, where elevation is the barycentric interpolation of the three position.y values and normal is a new THREE.Vector3 containing the normalized barycentric interpolation of their normals. If no triangle contains the point, return elevation 0 and normal (0,1,0). Shared-edge results must not depend on triangle traversal order.

2. Register a terrain-survey component usable on a-atoll-terrain. Its schema is radius (default 5), count (default 8), height (default 2), and markerRadius (default 0.2). On initialization, create one THREE.Group named survey-group containing count cylinder meshes named <component-id>-marker-k for k=0..count-1. For each marker, use angle=2*Math.PI*k/count, x=radius*cos(angle), and z=radius*sin(angle). Query getSurfaceInfo(x,z), put the cylinder base at (x,elevation,z) in terrain-local coordinates, align cylinder local +Y to the returned normal with setFromUnitVectors, and place its center at base + normal*(height/2). Use CylinderGeometry(markerRadius,markerRadius,height,12) and any vertex-color-compatible opaque material; no textures or remote assets.

3. When survey attributes update, rebuild only that component's marker group and dispose replaced marker geometries and materials. Do not regenerate or replace the terrain BufferGeometry. Sanitize updates as follows: non-finite radius, height, or markerRadius uses its default; finite radius is clamped to [0,10000], height to [0.1,100], and markerRadius to [0.01,10]. Count is rounded, clamped to [1,64], and non-finite count uses 8.

4. Add a deterministic demonstration to example.html using terrain id survey-terrain with seed="0.25", plateau-radius="6", plateau-elevation="2", middle-radius="20", unit-size="1", and far="80". Attach terrain-survey with id inner-survey, radius 4, count 8, height 2, and markerRadius 0.2. Add a second survey with id outer-survey, radius 30, count 4, height 3, and markerRadius 0.25. Keep the existing camera and movement controls usable.

5. Keep all coordinates in meters and all survey objects in the terrain entity's local space. The inner markers must therefore have base elevation 2 and center Y 3, while outer markers have base elevation 0 and center Y 1.5. Do not alter terrain seed, vertex positions, colors, or wave behavior when adding or updating surveys.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 60.0/100

Worker 超时、网页运行状态和质量分是独立记录。此处保留原始 VLM 评分；预览部署不改变评分。

维度分数理由
instruction2/4The terrain, survey components, update behavior, and demonstration are present, but the required exact surface query and inner-marker elevation contract fail. Four of eight inner markers are at Y=1 instead of Y=3, and slope interpolation disagrees with the live mesh beyond tolerance. This is a substantial central-flow defect rather than a minor omission.
interaction3/4Mouse-look and repeated attribute updates work reliably, with visible scene changes and correct count sanitation. Camera discoverability is limited to the canvas standard A-Frame interaction, but no promised survey UI is absent. The main interaction issue is data correctness rather than control responsiveness or recovery.
spatial2/4The initial and alternate camera views establish real terrain depth, scale, and occlusion, and survey cylinders are visible with correct dimensions. However, operation/inspection reveals incorrect placement of half the plateau markers and incorrect surface interpolation on sloped triangles, producing bases below the intended surface and mismatched normals.
stability3/4All tested repeated updates, boundary sanitation cases, canvas resize, keyboard focus, and reloads completed without crash. Identity checks showed the terrain and unaffected survey survived. Local issues are a favicon 404 and a sparse/clipping narrow layout, neither blocking the scene.
visual3/4The scene has a coherent bright-blue sky, textured green terrain, water, sun, and contrasting yellow survey markers. Composition is readable on desktop, though the narrow viewport is sparse and some marker bases are visually obscured by the spatial placement defect.

29. deterministic-wall-courses-and-fixed-step-replay

打开实际网页 ↗

修改题 · worker completed · 历史运行 PASS · 质量分 55.0/100

验收项 6 · 场景 4 · 必做 4 · 复杂度候选分位 0.801 · 原仓库代码文件 115

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Extend the existing Space Runner game with a deterministic course generator and a text-accessible replay interface. Preserve the existing title screen, wall and floor appearance, keyboard movement, Space pause/resume behavior, scoring counters, and normal webcam-driven play.

1. Add a seeded random function using a 32-bit LCG. Given an integer seed, initialize `state = seed >>> 0`; for each draw update `state = (Math.imul(1664525, state) + 1013904223) >>> 0` and return `state / 4294967296`. The default seed is 193. Do not use `Math.random()` for wall hole offsets or hole widths; unrelated wall colors may remain random.

2. For each wall, consume exactly three draws in this order: magnitude, sign, width. Compute `magnitude = Math.floor(r1 * 20) + 1`, `holeX = magnitude * (r2 < 0.5 ? -1 : 1)`, and `holeWidth = Math.ceil(r3 * 5) + 10`. Pass the resulting `holeX` and random function into `SingleHole` so its geometry uses that offset and width. Initial walls must remain at Z = 0, 100, ..., 900, and later batches must retain the existing 100-unit spacing and speed increase.

3. Expose `window.runnerDebug` as soon as the game script initializes, even when webcam permission is denied or poseNet never becomes ready. It must provide `resetCourse(seed)`, `getCourse()`, `getState()`, `setManualMode(enabled)`, and `step(count)`. These methods must not require keyboard events, webcam input, or a pose model.

4. `resetCourse(seed)` uses seed 193 when the argument is omitted, removes all existing wall and floor meshes, clears wall pools, and rebuilds one floor plus ten walls. Reset camera position to `(0,10,-35)`, camera Y rotation to `Math.PI`, head position to `(0,10,0)`, `cameraSpeed` to `0.25`, `cameraNoSpeed` to `0`, `wallCount` to `1`, `wallsPassed` and `level` to `0`, and `startFloor` to its post-creation value of 1500. Resetting must also enable manual mode.

5. `getCourse()` returns an array in course order containing `{index, z, holeX, holeWidth}` for every currently retained wall. `getState()` returns fresh values for camera position, head position, `cameraSpeed`, `wallCount`, `wallsPassed`, `level`, and `manualMode`; callers must not receive mutable internal position objects.

6. In manual mode, the animation loop may render but must not automatically advance or run gameplay progression. `step(count)` synchronously executes exactly `count` former gameplay iterations, including camera/head Z advancement, wall generation, counters, and level transitions, then renders once. Require an integer count from 1 through 10000; throw `RangeError` for any other value without changing state. Normal non-manual play remains frame-driven.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 55.0/100

Worker 超时、网页运行状态和质量分是独立记录。此处保留原始 VLM 评分；预览部署不改变评分。

维度分数理由
instruction2/4The debug interface, reset mechanics, fixed-step replay, and default-seed geometry are largely delivered, but a central requested flow is broken: different seeds produce the same course. The game's preserved presentation and keyboard start remain available.
spatial2/4The initial 3D course is recognizable and coherent, but examining progression exposes a core course-generation defect: changing the deterministic seed does not change wall hole geometry. This materially undermines the purpose of the spatial course system.
interaction3/4Fixed-step, mode switching, invalid-input rejection, and reset/replay sequences work reliably. Seed selection remains a local but requested failure, leaving the interaction rubric short of full compliance.
visual2/4The retro neon title and 3D course establish a readable desktop identity, but oversized centered copy overlaps the scene and development-console text. The narrow layout cuts off the title and instructions instead of reflowing.
stability2/4Desktop loading, reload, debug initialization, and repeated reset/step flows remain stable. The mobile presentation has a major responsive-layout failure, and the remote pose manifest timed out; the latter does not prevent the debug API from working.

30. deterministic-twisted-ring-transport-and-multi-ring-picking

打开实际网页 ↗

修改题 · worker completed · 历史运行 PASS · 质量分 91.2/100

验收项 7 · 场景 4 · 必做 4 · 复杂度候选分位 0.922 · 原仓库代码文件 40

题目及代码包（不含大型素材） · Rubric JSON · 历史评分 JSON

完整题面
Modify only `2018/00twisted-torus/index.html`, preserving the existing Three.js scene, RoundedBoxGeometry, three Ring configurations, materials, camera, OrbitControls, resize behavior, and rendering. Add the following deterministic animation and interaction features:

1. Replace frame-dependent rotation with a fixed-step simulation. Use `fixedDt = 1/60`, `simTime = 0`, `stepCount = 0`, `speed = 1`, and initially `running = true`. On every simulation step, for every box in all three rings apply `box.rotation.x -= 0.01 * speed`, then increment `stepCount` by 1 and `simTime` by `fixedDt`. In the animation loop, add `min((now - previousNow) / 1000, 0.1)` seconds to an accumulator and execute steps while the accumulator is at least `fixedDt`; subtract `fixedDt` after each step. While paused, do not accumulate time or change rotations, `simTime`, or `stepCount`.

2. Add a small HTML status panel showing running/paused, speed, simulation time to three decimal places, step count, and selected ring. Space toggles running/paused. The `[` and `]` keys move speed through the ordered values `[0.25, 0.5, 1, 2, 4]`, clamped at the endpoints. These keys must not reset simulation time or rotations.

3. Expose `window.torusLab` with `getState()`, `setRunning(value)`, `setSpeed(value)`, `step(n)`, and `reset()`. `getState()` returns `running`, `speed`, `simTime`, `stepCount`, `selectedRing`, and `rotations`, where `rotations` is an array of three arrays containing each ring's box X rotations in construction order. `setRunning` accepts only booleans. `setSpeed` accepts only the five listed numbers. Invalid calls leave all state unchanged. `step(n)` accepts only an integer from 0 through 600; it advances exactly `n` simulation steps only when paused and returns without changes otherwise.

4. Capture every box's initial X rotation after ring construction. `reset()` restores those rotations, sets `simTime = 0`, `stepCount = 0`, `speed = 1`, `running = false`, clears the accumulator, clears selection and hover highlighting, and restores any highlighted material's original emissive value. Reset must not rebuild geometry or alter camera and controls.

5. Extend raycasting to the boxes of all three rings, not just the first. Hovering a box sets its emissive to `0xff0000`; leaving it or moving to another box restores the previous box's saved emissive. A canvas click whose current raycast hits a box selects that box's ring, using IDs `ring`, `ring2`, and `ring3`; clicking empty space sets `selectedRing` to `null`. Selection itself must not pause, advance, or rotate the simulation.

6. Keep the implementation self-contained in the page and text-inspectable. Do not add assets, network services, randomness, or dependencies. The public API and status panel must remain functional after resize and after arbitrary sequences of pause, manual stepping, speed changes, picking, and reset.
历史五维评分与完整 Rubric · 91.2/100

Worker 超时、网页运行状态和质量分是独立记录。此处保留原始 VLM 评分；预览部署不改变评分。

维度分数理由
instruction3/4All principal flows are present and usable, but exact fixed-step time accumulation exceeds the stated tolerance and reset does not demonstrably restore a distinct post-construction rotation baseline. These are specific deviations from the requested numeric and reset contract.
interaction4/4Pause/resume, speed bounds, three-ring hover and selection, empty-space deselection, invalid stepping, reset, resize, and reload all responded reliably. Numeric timing fidelity is separately reflected in instruction and task checks.
spatial4/4The three rings have convincing rounded box geometry, visible depth, interleaving and occlusion, consistent gradient materials, and stable spatial presentation across camera views.
stability4/4The page loaded, resized, accepted keyboard input after resizing, and reloaded correctly. No overflow, crashes, or required-resource failures were observed; only a nonblocking favicon 404 appeared.
visual3/4The scene has a clear, attractive ring composition and legible status hierarchy. The help line wraps awkwardly, and the mobile view crops substantial portions of the outer rings, creating minor layout inconsistencies.
